import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import 'package:intl/date_symbol_data_local.dart'; // 銆愭柊澧炪€戝紩鍏ュ浗闄呭寲鏁版嵁鍒濆鍖栧寘
import 'package:monet_writer/providers/theme_provider.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/services/database_service.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/providers/user_provider.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/services/ai_service.dart';

// 椤甸潰寮曞叆
import 'package:monet_writer/pages/bookshelf/bookshelf_page.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/pages/profile_page.dart';

void main() async {
  // 1. 纭繚 Flutter 缁戝畾鍒濆鍖?
  WidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();

  // 2. 銆愬叧閿慨澶嶃€戝垵濮嬪寲涓枃鏃ユ湡鏍煎紡鏁版嵁
  // 杩欒浠ｇ爜瑙ｅ喅浜?"LocaleDataException: Locale data has not been initialized" 鎶ラ敊
  await initializeDateFormatting('zh_CN', null);

  // 3. 鍒濆鍖栨暟鎹簱鏈嶅姟
  await DatabaseService().init();

  // 4. 鍒濆鍖栫敤鎴锋暟鎹?(棰勫姞杞藉ご鍍忋€佹樀绉般€佽缃?
  final userProvider = UserProvider();
  await userProvider.loadUserData();

  // 5. 鍒濆鍖?AI 閰嶇疆 (棰勫姞杞?API Key)
  final aiProvider = AiProvider();
  await aiProvider.loadConfig();

  runApp(
    // 6. 娉ㄥ叆鍏ㄥ眬 Provider
    MultiProvider(
      providers: [
        // 涓婚绠＄悊
        ChangeNotifierProvider(create: (_) => ThemeProvider()),
        // 鐢ㄦ埛鏁版嵁绠＄悊 (浣跨敤宸插垵濮嬪寲鐨勫疄渚?
        ChangeNotifierProvider.value(value: userProvider),
        // AI 鏈嶅姟绠＄悊
        ChangeNotifierProvider.value(value: aiProvider),
      ],
      child: const MonetWriterApp(),
    ),
  );
}

class MonetWriterApp extends StatelessWidget {
  const MonetWriterApp({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // 鐩戝惉涓婚鍙樺寲
    final themeProvider = context.watch<ThemeProvider>();

    return MaterialApp(
      title: '钀界瑪',
      debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false, // 鍘婚櫎鍙充笂瑙?Debug 鏍囩

      // 娴呰壊涓婚
      theme: ThemeData(
        useMaterial3: true,
        colorScheme: ColorScheme.fromSeed(
          seedColor: themeProvider.seedColor,
          brightness: Brightness.light,
        ),
      ),

      // 娣辫壊涓婚
      darkTheme: ThemeData(
        useMaterial3: true,
        colorScheme: ColorScheme.fromSeed(
          seedColor: themeProvider.seedColor,
          brightness: Brightness.dark,
        ),
      ),

      // 璺熼殢绯荤粺鎴栫敤鎴疯缃殑涓婚妯″紡
      themeMode: themeProvider.themeMode,

      // App 鍏ュ彛椤甸潰
      home: const MainScaffold(),
    );
  }
}

/// 涓昏剼鎵嬫灦锛氳礋璐ｅ簳閮ㄥ鑸垏鎹?
class MainScaffold extends StatefulWidget {
  const MainScaffold({super.key});

  @override
  State<MainScaffold> createState() => _MainScaffoldState();
}

class _MainScaffoldState extends State<MainScaffold> {
  int _currentIndex = 0;

  // 椤甸潰鍒楄〃
  final List<Widget> _pages = [
    const BookshelfPage(), // 绱㈠紩 0: 鍐欎綔/涔︽灦
    const ProfilePage(),   // 绱㈠紩 1: 鎴戠殑/璁剧疆
  ];

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      // 鏄剧ず褰撳墠閫変腑鐨勯〉闈?
      body: _pages[_currentIndex],

      // 搴曢儴瀵艰埅鏍?(Material 3 椋庢牸)
      bottomNavigationBar: NavigationBar(
        selectedIndex: _currentIndex,
        onDestinationSelected: (index) {
          setState(() {
            _currentIndex = index;
          });
        },
        destinations: const [
          NavigationDestination(
            icon: Icon(Icons.book_outlined),
            selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.book),
            label: '鍐欎綔',
          ),
          NavigationDestination(
            icon: Icon(Icons.person_outline),
            selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.person),
            label: '鎴戠殑',
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:isar/isar.dart';

// 杩欎竴琛岀洰鍓嶄細鎶ラ敊锛岀◢鍚庤繍琛岀敓鎴愬懡浠や細鑷姩瑙ｅ喅
part 'schema.g.dart';

/// 涔︾睄妯″瀷
@collection
class Book {
  Id id = Isar.autoIncrement; // 鑷 ID

  @Index(type: IndexType.value)
  late String title; // 涔﹀悕

  String? author;    // 浣滆€呭悕
  String? desc;      // 绠€浠?

  // 灏侀潰涓昏壊璋?(Hex 瀛楃涓诧紝濡?"FF0000")
  // 鐢ㄤ簬鍦ㄥ垪琛ㄩ〉鏄剧ず婕備寒鐨勭函鑹茶儗鏅?
  String? coverColor;

  late DateTime createTime; // 鍒涘缓鏃堕棿
  late DateTime updateTime; // 鏇存柊鏃堕棿 (鐢ㄤ簬鎺掑簭)

  int totalWordCount = 0;   // 鎬诲瓧鏁?(缂撳瓨瀛楁锛岄伩鍏嶆瘡娆￠亶鍘嗙珷鑺傝绠?

  bool isFinished = false;  // 鐘舵€? false=杩炶浇涓? true=宸插畬缁?

  // 鍏宠仈锛氫竴鏈功鍖呭惈澶氫釜绔犺妭
  final chapters = IsarLinks<Chapter>();
}

/// 绔犺妭妯″瀷
@collection
class Chapter {
  Id id = Isar.autoIncrement;

  late String title;   // 绔犺妭鏍囬
  late String content; // 姝ｆ枃鍐呭

  int wordCount = 0;   // 鍗曠珷瀛楁暟

  late DateTime createTime;
  late DateTime updateTime;
}

  IsarCollection<Book> get books => this.collection();

const BookSchema = CollectionSchema(
  name: r'Book',
  id: 4089735379470416465,
  properties: {

      id: 0,
      name: r'author',
      type: IsarType.string,
    ),
    r'coverColor': PropertySchema(
      id: 1,
      name: r'coverColor',
      type: IsarType.string,
    ),
    r'createTime': PropertySchema(
      id: 2,
      name: r'createTime',
      type: IsarType.dateTime,
    ),
    r'desc': PropertySchema(
      id: 3,
      name: r'desc',
      type: IsarType.string,
    ),
    r'isFinished': PropertySchema(
      id: 4,
      name: r'isFinished',
      type: IsarType.bool,
    ),
    r'title': PropertySchema(
      id: 5,
      name: r'title',
      type: IsarType.string,
    ),
    r'totalWordCount': PropertySchema(
      id: 6,
      name: r'totalWordCount',
      type: IsarType.long,

      id: 7,
      name: r'updateTime',
      type: IsarType.dateTime,
    )
  },
  estimateSize: _bookEstimateSize,
  serialize: _bookSerialize,
  deserialize: _bookDeserialize,
  deserializeProp: _bookDeserializeProp,

    r'title': IndexSchema(
      id: -7636685945352118059,
      name: r'title',
      unique: false,
      replace: false,
      properties: [
        IndexPropertySchema(
          name: r'title',
          type: IndexType.value,
          caseSensitive: true,
        )
      ],
    )
  },
  links: {
    r'chapters': LinkSchema(
      id: -5332947351913554513,
      name: r'chapters',
      target: r'Chapter',
      single: false,
    )

  embeddedSchemas: {},
  getId: _bookGetId,

int _bookEstimateSize(
  Book object,
  List<int> offsets,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  var bytesCount = offsets.last;
  {
    final value = object.author;
    if (value != null) {
      bytesCount += 3 + value.length * 3;
    }
  }
  {
    final value = object.coverColor;
    if (value != null) {
      bytesCount += 3 + value.length * 3;
    }
  }
  {
    final value = object.desc;
    if (value != null) {
      bytesCount += 3 + value.length * 3;
    }
  }
  bytesCount += 3 + object.title.length * 3;
  return bytesCount;
}

void _bookSerialize(
  Book object,
  IsarWriter writer,
  List<int> offsets,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  writer.writeString(offsets[0], object.author);
  writer.writeString(offsets[1], object.coverColor);
  writer.writeDateTime(offsets[2], object.createTime);
  writer.writeString(offsets[3], object.desc);
  writer.writeBool(offsets[4], object.isFinished);
  writer.writeString(offsets[5], object.title);
  writer.writeLong(offsets[6], object.totalWordCount);
  writer.writeDateTime(offsets[7], object.updateTime);
}

Book _bookDeserialize(
  Id id,
  IsarReader reader,
  List<int> offsets,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  final object = Book();
  object.author = reader.readStringOrNull(offsets[0]);
  object.coverColor = reader.readStringOrNull(offsets[1]);
  object.createTime = reader.readDateTime(offsets[2]);
  object.desc = reader.readStringOrNull(offsets[3]);
  object.id = id;
  object.isFinished = reader.readBool(offsets[4]);
  object.title = reader.readString(offsets[5]);
  object.totalWordCount = reader.readLong(offsets[6]);
  object.updateTime = reader.readDateTime(offsets[7]);
  return object;
}

P _bookDeserializeProp<P>(
  IsarReader reader,
  int propertyId,
  int offset,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  switch (propertyId) {
    case 0:
      return (reader.readStringOrNull(offset)) as P;
    case 1:
      return (reader.readStringOrNull(offset)) as P;
    case 2:
      return (reader.readDateTime(offset)) as P;
    case 3:
      return (reader.readStringOrNull(offset)) as P;
    case 4:
      return (reader.readBool(offset)) as P;
    case 5:
      return (reader.readString(offset)) as P;
    case 6:
      return (reader.readLong(offset)) as P;
    case 7:
      return (reader.readDateTime(offset)) as P;
    default:
      throw IsarError('Unknown property with id $propertyId');
  }
}

Id _bookGetId(Book object) {
  return object.id;
}

List<IsarLinkBase<dynamic>> _bookGetLinks(Book object) {
  return [object.chapters];
}

void _bookAttach(IsarCollection<dynamic> col, Id id, Book object) {
  object.id = id;
  object.chapters.attach(col, col.isar.collection<Chapter>(), r'chapters', id);
}

extension BookQueryWhereSort on QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QWhere> {
  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterWhere> anyId() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(const IdWhereClause.any());
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterWhere> anyTitle() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(
        const IndexWhereClause.any(indexName: r'title'),
      );
    });
  }
}

extension BookQueryWhere on QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QWhereClause> {
  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterWhereClause> idEqualTo(Id id) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(IdWhereClause.between(
        lower: id,
        upper: id,
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterWhereClause> idNotEqualTo(Id id) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      if (query.whereSort == Sort.asc) {
        return query
            .addWhereClause(
              IdWhereClause.lessThan(upper: id, includeUpper: false),
            )
            .addWhereClause(
              IdWhereClause.greaterThan(lower: id, includeLower: false),
            );
      } else {
        return query
            .addWhereClause(
              IdWhereClause.greaterThan(lower: id, includeLower: false),
            )
            .addWhereClause(
              IdWhereClause.lessThan(upper: id, includeUpper: false),
            );
      }
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterWhereClause> idGreaterThan(Id id,
      {bool include = false}) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(
        IdWhereClause.greaterThan(lower: id, includeLower: include),
      );
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterWhereClause> idLessThan(Id id,
      {bool include = false}) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(
        IdWhereClause.lessThan(upper: id, includeUpper: include),
      );
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterWhereClause> idBetween(
    Id lowerId,
    Id upperId, {
    bool includeLower = true,
    bool includeUpper = true,
  }) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(IdWhereClause.between(
        lower: lowerId,
        includeLower: includeLower,
        upper: upperId,
        includeUpper: includeUpper,
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterWhereClause> titleEqualTo(String title) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(IndexWhereClause.equalTo(
        indexName: r'title',
        value: [title],
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterWhereClause> titleNotEqualTo(String title) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      if (query.whereSort == Sort.asc) {
        return query
            .addWhereClause(IndexWhereClause.between(
              indexName: r'title',
              lower: [],
              upper: [title],
              includeUpper: false,
            ))
            .addWhereClause(IndexWhereClause.between(
              indexName: r'title',
              lower: [title],
              includeLower: false,
              upper: [],
            ));
      } else {
        return query
            .addWhereClause(IndexWhereClause.between(
              indexName: r'title',
              lower: [title],
              includeLower: false,
              upper: [],
            ))
            .addWhereClause(IndexWhereClause.between(
              indexName: r'title',
              lower: [],
              upper: [title],
              includeUpper: false,
            ));
      }
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterWhereClause> titleGreaterThan(
    String title, {
    bool include = false,
  }) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(IndexWhereClause.between(
        indexName: r'title',
        lower: [title],
        includeLower: include,
        upper: [],
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterWhereClause> titleLessThan(
    String title, {
    bool include = false,
  }) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(IndexWhereClause.between(
        indexName: r'title',
        lower: [],
        upper: [title],
        includeUpper: include,
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterWhereClause> titleBetween(
    String lowerTitle,
    String upperTitle, {
    bool includeLower = true,
    bool includeUpper = true,
  }) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(IndexWhereClause.between(
        indexName: r'title',
        lower: [lowerTitle],
        includeLower: includeLower,
        upper: [upperTitle],
        includeUpper: includeUpper,
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterWhereClause> titleStartsWith(
      String TitlePrefix) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(IndexWhereClause.between(
        indexName: r'title',
        lower: [TitlePrefix],
        upper: ['$TitlePrefix\u{FFFFF}'],
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterWhereClause> titleIsEmpty() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(IndexWhereClause.equalTo(
        indexName: r'title',
        value: [''],
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterWhereClause> titleIsNotEmpty() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      if (query.whereSort == Sort.asc) {
        return query
            .addWhereClause(IndexWhereClause.lessThan(
              indexName: r'title',
              upper: [''],
            ))
            .addWhereClause(IndexWhereClause.greaterThan(
              indexName: r'title',
              lower: [''],
            ));
      } else {
        return query
            .addWhereClause(IndexWhereClause.greaterThan(
              indexName: r'title',
              lower: [''],
            ))
            .addWhereClause(IndexWhereClause.lessThan(
              indexName: r'title',
              upper: [''],
            ));
      }
    });
  }
}

extension BookQueryFilter on QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QFilterCondition> {
  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> authorIsNull() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addFilterCondition(const FilterCondition.isNull(
        property: r'author',
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> authorIsNotNull() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addFilterCondition(const FilterCondition.isNotNull(
        property: r'author',
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> authorEqualTo(
    String? value, {
    bool caseSensitive = true,
  }) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addFilterCondition(FilterCondition.equalTo(
        property: r'author',
        value: value,
        caseSensitive: caseSensitive,
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> authorGreaterThan(
    String? value, {
    bool include = false,
    bool caseSensitive = true,
  }) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addFilterCondition(FilterCondition.greaterThan(
        include: include,
        property: r'author',
        value: value,
        caseSensitive: caseSensitive,
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> authorLessThan(
    String? value, {
    bool include = false,
    bool caseSensitive = true,
  }) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addFilterCondition(FilterCondition.lessThan(
        include: include,
        property: r'author',
        value: value,
        caseSensitive: caseSensitive,
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> authorBetween(
    String? lower,
    String? upper, {
    bool includeLower = true,
    bool includeUpper = true,
    bool caseSensitive = true,
  }) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addFilterCondition(FilterCondition.between(
        property: r'author',
        lower: lower,
        includeLower: includeLower,
        upper: upper,
        includeUpper: includeUpper,
        caseSensitive: caseSensitive,
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> authorStartsWith(
    String value, {
    bool caseSensitive = true,
  }) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addFilterCondition(FilterCondition.startsWith(
        property: r'author',
        value: value,
        caseSensitive: caseSensitive,
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> authorEndsWith(
    String value, {
    bool caseSensitive = true,
  }) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addFilterCondition(FilterCondition.endsWith(
        property: r'author',
        value: value,
        caseSensitive: caseSensitive,
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> authorContains(String value,
      {bool caseSensitive = true}) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addFilterCondition(FilterCondition.contains(
        property: r'author',
        value: value,
        caseSensitive: caseSensitive,
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> authorMatches(String pattern,
      {bool caseSensitive = true}) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addFilterCondition(FilterCondition.matches(
        property: r'author',
        wildcard: pattern,
        caseSensitive: caseSensitive,
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> authorIsEmpty() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addFilterCondition(FilterCondition.equalTo(
        property: r'author',
        value: '',
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> authorIsNotEmpty() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addFilterCondition(FilterCondition.greaterThan(
        property: r'author',
        value: '',
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> coverColorIsNull() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addFilterCondition(const FilterCondition.isNull(
        property: r'coverColor',
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> coverColorIsNotNull() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addFilterCondition(const FilterCondition.isNotNull(
        property: r'coverColor',
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> coverColorEqualTo(
    String? value, {
    bool caseSensitive = true,
  }) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addFilterCondition(FilterCondition.equalTo(
        property: r'coverColor',
        value: value,
        caseSensitive: caseSensitive,
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> coverColorGreaterThan(
    String? value, {
    bool include = false,
    bool caseSensitive = true,
  }) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addFilterCondition(FilterCondition.greaterThan(
        include: include,
        property: r'coverColor',
        value: value,
        caseSensitive: caseSensitive,
      ));
    });

extension BookQueryObject on QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QFilterCondition> {}

extension BookQueryLinks on QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QFilterCondition> {
  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> chapters(
      FilterQuery<Chapter> q) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.link(q, r'chapters');
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> chaptersLengthEqualTo(
      int length) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.linkLength(r'chapters', length, true, length, true);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> chaptersIsEmpty() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.linkLength(r'chapters', 0, true, 0, true);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> chaptersIsNotEmpty() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.linkLength(r'chapters', 0, false, 999999, true);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> chaptersLengthLessThan(
    int length, {
    bool include = false,
  }) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.linkLength(r'chapters', 0, true, length, include);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> chaptersLengthGreaterThan(
    int length, {
    bool include = false,
  }) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.linkLength(r'chapters', length, include, 999999, true);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> chaptersLengthBetween(
    int lower,
    int upper, {
    bool includeLower = true,
    bool includeUpper = true,
  }) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.linkLength(
          r'chapters', lower, includeLower, upper, includeUpper);
    });
  }
}

extension GetChapterCollection on Isar {
  IsarCollection<Chapter> get chapters => this.collection();
}

const ChapterSchema = CollectionSchema(
  name: r'Chapter',
  id: -7604549436611156012,
  properties: {
    r'content': PropertySchema(
      id: 0,
      name: r'content',
      type: IsarType.string,
    ),
    r'createTime': PropertySchema(
      id: 1,
      name: r'createTime',
      type: IsarType.dateTime,
    ),
    r'title': PropertySchema(
      id: 2,
      name: r'title',
      type: IsarType.string,
    ),
    r'updateTime': PropertySchema(
      id: 3,
      name: r'updateTime',
      type: IsarType.dateTime,
    ),
    r'wordCount': PropertySchema(
      id: 4,
      name: r'wordCount',
      type: IsarType.long,
    )
  },
  estimateSize: _chapterEstimateSize,
  serialize: _chapterSerialize,
  deserialize: _chapterDeserialize,
  deserializeProp: _chapterDeserializeProp,
  idName: r'id',
  indexes: {},
  links: {},
  embeddedSchemas: {},
  getId: _chapterGetId,
  getLinks: _chapterGetLinks,
  attach: _chapterAttach,
  version: '3.1.0+1',
);

int _chapterEstimateSize(
  Chapter object,
  List<int> offsets,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  var bytesCount = offsets.last;
  bytesCount += 3 + object.content.length * 3;
  bytesCount += 3 + object.title.length * 3;
  return bytesCount;
}

void _chapterSerialize(
  Chapter object,
  IsarWriter writer,
  List<int> offsets,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  writer.writeString(offsets[0], object.content);
  writer.writeDateTime(offsets[1], object.createTime);
  writer.writeString(offsets[2], object.title);
  writer.writeDateTime(offsets[3], object.updateTime);
  writer.writeLong(offsets[4], object.wordCount);
}

Chapter _chapterDeserialize(
  Id id,
  IsarReader reader,
  List<int> offsets,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  final object = Chapter();
  object.content = reader.readString(offsets[0]);
  object.createTime = reader.readDateTime(offsets[1]);
  object.id = id;
  object.title = reader.readString(offsets[2]);
  object.updateTime = reader.readDateTime(offsets[3]);
  object.wordCount = reader.readLong(offsets[4]);
  return object;
}

P _chapterDeserializeProp<P>(
  IsarReader reader,
  int propertyId,
  int offset,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  switch (propertyId) {
    case 0:
      return (reader.readString(offset)) as P;
    case 1:
      return (reader.readDateTime(offset)) as P;
    case 2:
      return (reader.readString(offset)) as P;
    case 3:
      return (reader.readDateTime(offset)) as P;
    case 4:
      return (reader.readLong(offset)) as P;
    default:
      throw IsarError('Unknown property with id $propertyId');
  }
}

Id _chapterGetId(Chapter object) {
  return object.id;
}

List<IsarLinkBase<dynamic>> _chapterGetLinks(Chapter object) {
  return [];
}

void _chapterAttach(IsarCollection<dynamic> col, Id id, Chapter object) {
  object.id = id;
}

extension ChapterQueryWhereSort on QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QWhere> {
  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterWhere> anyId() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(const IdWhereClause.any());
    });
  }
}

extension ChapterQueryWhere on QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QWhereClause> {
  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterWhereClause> idEqualTo(Id id) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(IdWhereClause.between(
        lower: id,
        upper: id,
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterWhereClause> idNotEqualTo(Id id) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      if (query.whereSort == Sort.asc) {
        return query
            .addWhereClause(
              IdWhereClause.lessThan(upper: id, includeUpper: false),
            )
            .addWhereClause(
              IdWhereClause.greaterThan(lower: id, includeLower: false),
            );
      } else {
        return query
            .addWhereClause(
              IdWhereClause.greaterThan(lower: id, includeLower: false),
            )
            .addWhereClause(
              IdWhereClause.lessThan(upper: id, includeUpper: false),
            );
      }
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterWhereClause> idGreaterThan(Id id,
      {bool include = false}) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(
        IdWhereClause.greaterThan(lower: id, includeLower: include),
      );
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterWhereClause> idLessThan(Id id,
      {bool include = false}) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(
        IdWhereClause.lessThan(upper: id, includeUpper: include),
      );
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterWhereClause> idBetween(
    Id lowerId,
    Id upperId, {
    bool includeLower = true,
    bool includeUpper = true,
  }) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(IdWhereClause.between(
        lower: lowerId,
        includeLower: includeLower,
        upper: upperId,
        includeUpper: includeUpper,
      ));
    });
  }
}

import 'package:isar/isar.dart';

part 'ai_config.g.dart';

@collection
class AiConfig {
  Id id = Isar.autoIncrement;

  /// 鏈嶅姟鍟? google, openai, silicon, deepseek
  String provider = 'google';

  /// API Key
  String apiKey = '';

  /// Base URL (OpenAI 鏍煎紡鎺ュ彛涓撶敤)
  String baseUrl = '';

  /// 妯″瀷鍚嶇О (濡?gemini-pro, deepseek-chat)
  String modelName = '';

  /// 銆愬叧閿慨澶嶃€戞柊澧炲瓧娈碉紝瑙ｅ喅 getter not defined 鎶ラ敊
  String? customPrompt;
}

  AiConfig object,
  List<int> offsets,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  var bytesCount = offsets.last;
  bytesCount += 3 + object.apiKey.length * 3;
  bytesCount += 3 + object.baseUrl.length * 3;
  {

    if (value != null) {
      bytesCount += 3 + value.length * 3;
    }
  }
  bytesCount += 3 + object.modelName.length * 3;
  bytesCount += 3 + object.provider.length * 3;
  return bytesCount;
}

  object.provider = reader.readString(offsets[4]);
  return object;
}

    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      if (query.whereSort == Sort.asc) {
        return query
            .addWhereClause(
              IdWhereClause.lessThan(upper: id, includeUpper: false),
            )

            );
      } else {
        return query
            .addWhereClause(
              IdWhereClause.greaterThan(lower: id, includeLower: false),
            )
            .addWhereClause(
              IdWhereClause.lessThan(upper: id, includeUpper: false),
            );
      }
    });
  }

import 'package:isar/isar.dart';
import 'custom_outline.dart';
import 'outline_group.dart';
import 'character.dart';
import 'character_group.dart';
import 'outline_tab.dart';
import 'outline_node.dart';
// 銆愬叧閿慨澶嶃€戝繀椤绘樉寮忓紩鍏ヨ繖涓枃浠讹紝Book 鎵嶈兘璇嗗埆鍒版繁灞傚祵濂楃殑 CharacterEventSchema
import 'character_event.dart';

part 'book.g.dart';

@collection
class Book {
  Id id = Isar.autoIncrement;

  late String title;
  String? coverPath;
  String? description; // 鏍稿績姊楁 (Logline)

  // 浣滆€?绗斿悕
  String? authorName;

  // 鐘舵€侊細0=杩炶浇涓? 1=宸插畬缁?
  int status = 0;

  late DateTime createdAt;
  late DateTime updatedAt;

  int wordCount = 0;
  bool isDeleted = false;
  int? lastChapterId;

  // --- 澶х翰妯″潡 ---

  String? outline; // 鏍稿績鎬荤翰
  String? volumeOutline; // (鏆傜暀锛屾湭鏉ュ彲鑳藉悎骞?

  // 涓荤嚎鍓ф儏 (瀵瑰簲 "鍓ф儏路缁嗙翰" Tab)
  List<CustomOutline>? customOutlines;

  // (鏃? 澶х翰鍒嗙粍 - 鐣欑潃鍏煎闃叉鎶ラ敊锛屽悗缁竻鐞?
  List<OutlineGroup>? outlineGroups;

  // 鑷畾涔夎瀹氶泦 (瀵瑰簲 "瀹忚路璁惧畾" 涓嬬殑鑷畾涔?Tabs)
  List<OutlineTab>? settingsTabs;

  // --- 瑙掕壊绠＄悊妯″潡 ---

  /// 鏍圭洰褰曚笅鐨勮鑹?
  List<Character>? characters;

  /// 瑙掕壊鍒嗙粍
  List<CharacterGroup>? characterGroups;
}

int _bookEstimateSize(
  Book object,
  List<int> offsets,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  var bytesCount = offsets.last;
  {
    final value = object.authorName;
    if (value != null) {
      bytesCount += 3 + value.length * 3;
    }
  }
  {
    final list = object.characterGroups;
    if (list != null) {
      bytesCount += 3 + list.length * 3;
      {
        final offsets = allOffsets[CharacterGroup]!;
        for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
          final value = list[i];
          bytesCount +=
              CharacterGroupSchema.estimateSize(value, offsets, allOffsets);
        }
      }
    }
  }
  {
    final list = object.characters;
    if (list != null) {
      bytesCount += 3 + list.length * 3;
      {
        final offsets = allOffsets[Character]!;
        for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
          final value = list[i];
          bytesCount +=
              CharacterSchema.estimateSize(value, offsets, allOffsets);
        }
      }
    }
  }
  {
    final value = object.coverPath;
    if (value != null) {
      bytesCount += 3 + value.length * 3;
    }
  }
  {
    final list = object.customOutlines;
    if (list != null) {
      bytesCount += 3 + list.length * 3;
      {
        final offsets = allOffsets[CustomOutline]!;
        for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
          final value = list[i];
          bytesCount +=
              CustomOutlineSchema.estimateSize(value, offsets, allOffsets);
        }
      }
    }
  }
  {
    final value = object.description;
    if (value != null) {
      bytesCount += 3 + value.length * 3;
    }
  }
  {
    final value = object.outline;
    if (value != null) {
      bytesCount += 3 + value.length * 3;
    }
  }
  {
    final list = object.outlineGroups;
    if (list != null) {
      bytesCount += 3 + list.length * 3;
      {
        final offsets = allOffsets[OutlineGroup]!;
        for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
          final value = list[i];
          bytesCount +=
              OutlineGroupSchema.estimateSize(value, offsets, allOffsets);
        }
      }
    }
  }
  {
    final list = object.settingsTabs;
    if (list != null) {
      bytesCount += 3 + list.length * 3;
      {
        final offsets = allOffsets[OutlineTab]!;
        for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
          final value = list[i];
          bytesCount +=
              OutlineTabSchema.estimateSize(value, offsets, allOffsets);
        }
      }
    }
  }
  bytesCount += 3 + object.title.length * 3;
  {
    final value = object.volumeOutline;
    if (value != null) {
      bytesCount += 3 + value.length * 3;
    }
  }
  return bytesCount;
}

void _bookSerialize(
  Book object,
  IsarWriter writer,
  List<int> offsets,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  writer.writeString(offsets[0], object.authorName);
  writer.writeObjectList<CharacterGroup>(
    offsets[1],
    allOffsets,
    CharacterGroupSchema.serialize,
    object.characterGroups,
  );
  writer.writeObjectList<Character>(
    offsets[2],
    allOffsets,
    CharacterSchema.serialize,
    object.characters,
  );
  writer.writeString(offsets[3], object.coverPath);
  writer.writeDateTime(offsets[4], object.createdAt);
  writer.writeObjectList<CustomOutline>(
    offsets[5],
    allOffsets,
    CustomOutlineSchema.serialize,
    object.customOutlines,
  );
  writer.writeString(offsets[6], object.description);
  writer.writeBool(offsets[7], object.isDeleted);
  writer.writeLong(offsets[8], object.lastChapterId);
  writer.writeString(offsets[9], object.outline);
  writer.writeObjectList<OutlineGroup>(
    offsets[10],
    allOffsets,
    OutlineGroupSchema.serialize,
    object.outlineGroups,
  );
  writer.writeObjectList<OutlineTab>(
    offsets[11],
    allOffsets,
    OutlineTabSchema.serialize,
    object.settingsTabs,
  );
  writer.writeLong(offsets[12], object.status);
  writer.writeString(offsets[13], object.title);
  writer.writeDateTime(offsets[14], object.updatedAt);
  writer.writeString(offsets[15], object.volumeOutline);
  writer.writeLong(offsets[16], object.wordCount);
}

Book _bookDeserialize(
  Id id,
  IsarReader reader,
  List<int> offsets,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  final object = Book();
  object.authorName = reader.readStringOrNull(offsets[0]);
  object.characterGroups = reader.readObjectList<CharacterGroup>(
    offsets[1],
    CharacterGroupSchema.deserialize,
    allOffsets,
    CharacterGroup(),
  );
  object.characters = reader.readObjectList<Character>(
    offsets[2],
    CharacterSchema.deserialize,
    allOffsets,
    Character(),
  );
  object.coverPath = reader.readStringOrNull(offsets[3]);
  object.createdAt = reader.readDateTime(offsets[4]);
  object.customOutlines = reader.readObjectList<CustomOutline>(
    offsets[5],
    CustomOutlineSchema.deserialize,
    allOffsets,
    CustomOutline(),
  );
  object.description = reader.readStringOrNull(offsets[6]);
  object.id = id;
  object.isDeleted = reader.readBool(offsets[7]);
  object.lastChapterId = reader.readLongOrNull(offsets[8]);
  object.outline = reader.readStringOrNull(offsets[9]);
  object.outlineGroups = reader.readObjectList<OutlineGroup>(
    offsets[10],
    OutlineGroupSchema.deserialize,
    allOffsets,
    OutlineGroup(),
  );
  object.settingsTabs = reader.readObjectList<OutlineTab>(
    offsets[11],
    OutlineTabSchema.deserialize,
    allOffsets,
    OutlineTab(),
  );
  object.status = reader.readLong(offsets[12]);
  object.title = reader.readString(offsets[13]);
  object.updatedAt = reader.readDateTime(offsets[14]);
  object.volumeOutline = reader.readStringOrNull(offsets[15]);
  object.wordCount = reader.readLong(offsets[16]);
  return object;
}

P _bookDeserializeProp<P>(
  IsarReader reader,
  int propertyId,
  int offset,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  switch (propertyId) {
    case 0:
      return (reader.readStringOrNull(offset)) as P;
    case 1:
      return (reader.readObjectList<CharacterGroup>(
        offset,
        CharacterGroupSchema.deserialize,
        allOffsets,
        CharacterGroup(),
      )) as P;
    case 2:
      return (reader.readObjectList<Character>(
        offset,
        CharacterSchema.deserialize,
        allOffsets,
        Character(),
      )) as P;
    case 3:
      return (reader.readStringOrNull(offset)) as P;
    case 4:
      return (reader.readDateTime(offset)) as P;
    case 5:
      return (reader.readObjectList<CustomOutline>(
        offset,
        CustomOutlineSchema.deserialize,
        allOffsets,
        CustomOutline(),
      )) as P;
    case 6:
      return (reader.readStringOrNull(offset)) as P;
    case 7:
      return (reader.readBool(offset)) as P;
    case 8:
      return (reader.readLongOrNull(offset)) as P;
    case 9:
      return (reader.readStringOrNull(offset)) as P;
    case 10:
      return (reader.readObjectList<OutlineGroup>(
        offset,
        OutlineGroupSchema.deserialize,
        allOffsets,
        OutlineGroup(),
      )) as P;
    case 11:
      return (reader.readObjectList<OutlineTab>(
        offset,
        OutlineTabSchema.deserialize,
        allOffsets,
        OutlineTab(),
      )) as P;
    case 12:
      return (reader.readLong(offset)) as P;
    case 13:
      return (reader.readString(offset)) as P;
    case 14:
      return (reader.readDateTime(offset)) as P;
    case 15:
      return (reader.readStringOrNull(offset)) as P;
    case 16:
      return (reader.readLong(offset)) as P;
    default:
      throw IsarError('Unknown property with id $propertyId');
  }
}

Id _bookGetId(Book object) {
  return object.id;
}

List<IsarLinkBase<dynamic>> _bookGetLinks(Book object) {
  return [];
}

void _bookAttach(IsarCollection<dynamic> col, Id id, Book object) {
  object.id = id;
}

extension BookQueryWhereSort on QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QWhere> {
  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterWhere> anyId() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(const IdWhereClause.any());
    });
  }
}

extension BookQueryWhere on QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QWhereClause> {
  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterWhereClause> idEqualTo(Id id) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(IdWhereClause.between(
        lower: id,
        upper: id,
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterWhereClause> idNotEqualTo(Id id) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      if (query.whereSort == Sort.asc) {
        return query
            .addWhereClause(
              IdWhereClause.lessThan(upper: id, includeUpper: false),
            )
            .addWhereClause(
              IdWhereClause.greaterThan(lower: id, includeLower: false),
            );
      } else {
        return query
            .addWhereClause(
              IdWhereClause.greaterThan(lower: id, includeLower: false),
            )
            .addWhereClause(
              IdWhereClause.lessThan(upper: id, includeUpper: false),
            );
      }
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterWhereClause> idGreaterThan(Id id,
      {bool include = false}) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(
        IdWhereClause.greaterThan(lower: id, includeLower: include),
      );
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterWhereClause> idLessThan(Id id,
      {bool include = false}) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(
        IdWhereClause.lessThan(upper: id, includeUpper: include),
      );
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterWhereClause> idBetween(
    Id lowerId,
    Id upperId, {
    bool includeLower = true,
    bool includeUpper = true,
  }) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(IdWhereClause.between(
        lower: lowerId,
        includeLower: includeLower,
        upper: upperId,
        includeUpper: includeUpper,
      ));
    });
  }
}

extension BookQueryObject on QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QFilterCondition> {
  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> characterGroupsElement(
      FilterQuery<CharacterGroup> q) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.object(q, r'characterGroups');
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> charactersElement(
      FilterQuery<Character> q) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.object(q, r'characters');
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> customOutlinesElement(
      FilterQuery<CustomOutline> q) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.object(q, r'customOutlines');
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> outlineGroupsElement(
      FilterQuery<OutlineGroup> q) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.object(q, r'outlineGroups');
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterFilterCondition> settingsTabsElement(
      FilterQuery<OutlineTab> q) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.object(q, r'settingsTabs');
    });
  }
}

extension BookQueryLinks on QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QFilterCondition> {}

extension BookQuerySortBy on QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QSortBy> {
  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterSortBy> sortByAuthorName() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'authorName', Sort.asc);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterSortBy> sortByAuthorNameDesc() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'authorName', Sort.desc);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Book, Book, QAfterSortBy> sortByCoverPath() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'coverPath', Sort.asc);
    });

import 'package:isar/isar.dart';

part 'chapter.g.dart';

@collection
class Chapter {
  Id id = Isar.autoIncrement;

  @Index()
  late int bookId;

  late String title;

  late String content;

  // 銆愭柊澧炪€戞湰绔犵粏绾?
  String? outline;

  int wordCount = 0;

  late int orderIndex;

  late DateTime updatedAt;
}

int _chapterEstimateSize(
  Chapter object,
  List<int> offsets,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  var bytesCount = offsets.last;
  bytesCount += 3 + object.content.length * 3;
  {
    final value = object.outline;
    if (value != null) {
      bytesCount += 3 + value.length * 3;
    }
  }
  bytesCount += 3 + object.title.length * 3;
  return bytesCount;
}

void _chapterSerialize(
  Chapter object,
  IsarWriter writer,
  List<int> offsets,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  writer.writeLong(offsets[0], object.bookId);
  writer.writeString(offsets[1], object.content);
  writer.writeLong(offsets[2], object.orderIndex);
  writer.writeString(offsets[3], object.outline);
  writer.writeString(offsets[4], object.title);
  writer.writeDateTime(offsets[5], object.updatedAt);
  writer.writeLong(offsets[6], object.wordCount);
}

Chapter _chapterDeserialize(
  Id id,
  IsarReader reader,
  List<int> offsets,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  final object = Chapter();
  object.bookId = reader.readLong(offsets[0]);
  object.content = reader.readString(offsets[1]);
  object.id = id;
  object.orderIndex = reader.readLong(offsets[2]);
  object.outline = reader.readStringOrNull(offsets[3]);
  object.title = reader.readString(offsets[4]);
  object.updatedAt = reader.readDateTime(offsets[5]);
  object.wordCount = reader.readLong(offsets[6]);
  return object;
}

P _chapterDeserializeProp<P>(
  IsarReader reader,
  int propertyId,
  int offset,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  switch (propertyId) {
    case 0:
      return (reader.readLong(offset)) as P;
    case 1:
      return (reader.readString(offset)) as P;
    case 2:
      return (reader.readLong(offset)) as P;
    case 3:
      return (reader.readStringOrNull(offset)) as P;
    case 4:
      return (reader.readString(offset)) as P;
    case 5:
      return (reader.readDateTime(offset)) as P;
    case 6:
      return (reader.readLong(offset)) as P;
    default:
      throw IsarError('Unknown property with id $propertyId');
  }
}

extension ChapterQueryWhere on QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QWhereClause> {
  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterWhereClause> idEqualTo(Id id) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(IdWhereClause.between(
        lower: id,
        upper: id,
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterWhereClause> idNotEqualTo(Id id) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      if (query.whereSort == Sort.asc) {
        return query
            .addWhereClause(
              IdWhereClause.lessThan(upper: id, includeUpper: false),
            )
            .addWhereClause(
              IdWhereClause.greaterThan(lower: id, includeLower: false),
            );
      } else {
        return query
            .addWhereClause(
              IdWhereClause.greaterThan(lower: id, includeLower: false),
            )
            .addWhereClause(
              IdWhereClause.lessThan(upper: id, includeUpper: false),
            );
      }
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterWhereClause> idGreaterThan(Id id,
      {bool include = false}) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(
        IdWhereClause.greaterThan(lower: id, includeLower: include),
      );
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterWhereClause> idLessThan(Id id,
      {bool include = false}) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(
        IdWhereClause.lessThan(upper: id, includeUpper: include),
      );
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterWhereClause> idBetween(
    Id lowerId,
    Id upperId, {
    bool includeLower = true,
    bool includeUpper = true,
  }) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(IdWhereClause.between(
        lower: lowerId,
        includeLower: includeLower,
        upper: upperId,
        includeUpper: includeUpper,
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterWhereClause> bookIdEqualTo(int bookId) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(IndexWhereClause.equalTo(
        indexName: r'bookId',
        value: [bookId],
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterWhereClause> bookIdNotEqualTo(
      int bookId) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      if (query.whereSort == Sort.asc) {
        return query
            .addWhereClause(IndexWhereClause.between(
              indexName: r'bookId',
              lower: [],
              upper: [bookId],
              includeUpper: false,
            ))
            .addWhereClause(IndexWhereClause.between(
              indexName: r'bookId',
              lower: [bookId],
              includeLower: false,
              upper: [],
            ));
      } else {
        return query
            .addWhereClause(IndexWhereClause.between(
              indexName: r'bookId',
              lower: [bookId],
              includeLower: false,
              upper: [],
            ))
            .addWhereClause(IndexWhereClause.between(
              indexName: r'bookId',
              lower: [],
              upper: [bookId],
              includeUpper: false,
            ));
      }
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterWhereClause> bookIdGreaterThan(
    int bookId, {
    bool include = false,
  }) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(IndexWhereClause.between(
        indexName: r'bookId',
        lower: [bookId],
        includeLower: include,
        upper: [],
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterWhereClause> bookIdLessThan(
    int bookId, {
    bool include = false,
  }) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(IndexWhereClause.between(
        indexName: r'bookId',
        lower: [],
        upper: [bookId],
        includeUpper: include,
      ));
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterWhereClause> bookIdBetween(
    int lowerBookId,
    int upperBookId, {
    bool includeLower = true,
    bool includeUpper = true,
  }) {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addWhereClause(IndexWhereClause.between(
        indexName: r'bookId',
        lower: [lowerBookId],
        includeLower: includeLower,
        upper: [upperBookId],
        includeUpper: includeUpper,
      ));
    });
  }
}

extension ChapterQuerySortBy on QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QSortBy> {
  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterSortBy> sortByBookId() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'bookId', Sort.asc);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterSortBy> sortByBookIdDesc() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'bookId', Sort.desc);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterSortBy> sortByContent() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'content', Sort.asc);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterSortBy> sortByContentDesc() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'content', Sort.desc);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterSortBy> sortByOrderIndex() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'orderIndex', Sort.asc);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterSortBy> sortByOrderIndexDesc() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'orderIndex', Sort.desc);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterSortBy> sortByOutline() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'outline', Sort.asc);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterSortBy> sortByOutlineDesc() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'outline', Sort.desc);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterSortBy> sortByTitle() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'title', Sort.asc);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterSortBy> sortByTitleDesc() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'title', Sort.desc);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterSortBy> sortByUpdatedAt() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'updatedAt', Sort.asc);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<Chapter, Chapter, QAfterSortBy> sortByUpdatedAtDesc() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'updatedAt', Sort.desc);

import 'package:isar/isar.dart';
import 'character_event.dart'; // 銆愭柊澧炪€戝紩鍏ヤ簨浠舵ā鍨?

part 'character.g.dart';

/// 瑙掕壊妯″瀷
/// 杩欐槸涓€涓祵鍏ュ紡瀵硅薄锛屽瓨鏀惧湪 Book 鎴?CharacterGroup 涓?
@embedded
class Character {
  /// 瑙掕壊鍚嶇О
  String? name;

  /// 澶村儚璺緞
  String? avatarPath;

  /// 瑙掕壊瀹氫綅/韬唤
  String? role;

  /// 涓€鍙ヨ瘽绠€浠?
  String? description;

  /// 鎺掑簭绱㈠紩
  int orderIndex = 0;

  // --- 璇︽儏椤垫暟鎹?---

  /// 璇︾粏鐢熷钩 (鎬绘嫭/璁惧畾鏂囨)
  String? bio;

  /// 鏍囩鍒楄〃
  List<String>? tags;

  /// 銆愭柊澧炪€戠敓骞充簨浠舵椂闂磋酱
  List<CharacterEvent>? lifeEvents;
}

    on QueryBuilder<Character, Character, QFilterCondition> {

import 'package:isar/isar.dart';

part 'character_event.g.dart';

@embedded
class CharacterEvent {
  String? title;       // 浜嬩欢姒傝 (濡傦細瀹舵棌鐏棬)
  String? content;     // 璇︾粏鎻忚堪
  String? timePoint;   // 鏃堕棿鐐?(濡傦細瀹ｅ拰涓夊勾锛屾垨 18宀?
  String? chapterSource; // 鏉ユ簮绔犺妭 (鍙€夛紝鐢ㄤ簬婧簮)

  // 杈呭姪鏋勯€?
  CharacterEvent({
    this.title,
    this.content,
    this.timePoint,
    this.chapterSource,
  });
}

import 'package:isar/isar.dart';

part 'character_group.g.dart';

/// 瑙掕壊鍒嗙粍妯″瀷 (绫讳技鏂囦欢澶?
@embedded
class CharacterGroup {
  /// 鍒嗙粍鏍囬 (濡? 涓昏鍥? 鏁屽鍔垮姏)
  String? title;

  /// 缁勫唴鐨勮鑹插垪琛?
  List<Character>? characters;

  /// 杈呭姪鏂规硶锛氳幏鍙栧畨鍏ㄧ殑鍒楄〃 (闃叉 null)
  List<Character> get safeCharacters => characters ?? [];
}

  name: r'CustomOutline',
  id: 3897348390016408361,
  properties: {
    r'content': PropertySchema(
      id: 0,
      name: r'content',
      type: IsarType.string,
    ),
    r'title': PropertySchema(
      id: 1,
      name: r'title',
      type: IsarType.string,
    )
  },
  estimateSize: _customOutlineEstimateSize,
  serialize: _customOutlineSerialize,
  deserialize: _customOutlineDeserialize,
  deserializeProp: _customOutlineDeserializeProp,
);

int _customOutlineEstimateSize(
  CustomOutline object,
  List<int> offsets,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  var bytesCount = offsets.last;
  {
    final value = object.content;
    if (value != null) {
      bytesCount += 3 + value.length * 3;
    }
  }
  {
    final value = object.title;
    if (value != null) {
      bytesCount += 3 + value.length * 3;
    }
  }
  return bytesCount;
}

void _customOutlineSerialize(
  CustomOutline object,
  IsarWriter writer,
  List<int> offsets,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  writer.writeString(offsets[0], object.content);
  writer.writeString(offsets[1], object.title);
}

CustomOutline _customOutlineDeserialize(
  Id id,
  IsarReader reader,
  List<int> offsets,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  final object = CustomOutline();
  object.content = reader.readStringOrNull(offsets[0]);
  object.title = reader.readStringOrNull(offsets[1]);
  return object;
}

P _customOutlineDeserializeProp<P>(
  IsarReader reader,
  int propertyId,
  int offset,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  switch (propertyId) {
    case 0:
      return (reader.readStringOrNull(offset)) as P;
    case 1:
      return (reader.readStringOrNull(offset)) as P;
    default:
      throw IsarError('Unknown property with id $propertyId');
  }
}

import 'package:isar/isar.dart';

part 'daily_stats.g.dart';

@collection
class DailyStats {
  Id id = Isar.autoIncrement;

  /// 鏃ユ湡 (鍙湁骞存湀鏃ワ紝鏃跺垎绉掍负0)
  @Index(unique: true)
  late DateTime date;

  /// 褰撴棩鎬荤爜瀛楁暟 (鍙樺寲閲?
  int wordCount = 0;

  /// 鏈€鍚庢洿鏂版椂闂?
  late DateTime updatedAt;

  /// 銆愭柊澧炪€戝綋鏃ユ椿鍔ㄨ鎯呰褰?
  List<DailyLog> logs = [];
}

/// 銆愭柊澧炪€戝祵鍏ュ紡瀵硅薄锛岃褰曞叿浣撶殑绔犺妭淇敼
@embedded
class DailyLog {
  String? timeStr;      // 璁板綍鏃堕棿鐐?(濡?"14:30")
  String? bookTitle;    // 涔﹀悕
  String? chapterTitle; // 绔犺妭鍚?
  int wordCountChange = 0; // 璇ョ珷鑺備粖鏃ョ殑瀛楁暟鍙樺寲
}

int _dailyStatsEstimateSize(
  DailyStats object,
  List<int> offsets,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  var bytesCount = offsets.last;
  bytesCount += 3 + object.logs.length * 3;
  {
    final offsets = allOffsets[DailyLog]!;
    for (var i = 0; i < object.logs.length; i++) {
      final value = object.logs[i];
      bytesCount += DailyLogSchema.estimateSize(value, offsets, allOffsets);
    }
  }
  return bytesCount;
}

void _dailyStatsSerialize(
  DailyStats object,
  IsarWriter writer,
  List<int> offsets,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  writer.writeDateTime(offsets[0], object.date);
  writer.writeObjectList<DailyLog>(
    offsets[1],
    allOffsets,
    DailyLogSchema.serialize,
    object.logs,
  );
  writer.writeDateTime(offsets[2], object.updatedAt);
  writer.writeLong(offsets[3], object.wordCount);
}

  List<int> offsets,

  final object = DailyStats();

        allOffsets,
        DailyLog(),

  object.updatedAt = reader.readDateTime(offsets[2]);
  object.wordCount = reader.readLong(offsets[3]);
  return object;
}

const DailyLogSchema = Schema(
  name: r'DailyLog',
  id: -3995615497450705259,
  properties: {
    r'bookTitle': PropertySchema(
      id: 0,
      name: r'bookTitle',
      type: IsarType.string,
    ),
    r'chapterTitle': PropertySchema(
      id: 1,
      name: r'chapterTitle',
      type: IsarType.string,
    ),
    r'timeStr': PropertySchema(
      id: 2,
      name: r'timeStr',
      type: IsarType.string,
    ),
    r'wordCountChange': PropertySchema(
      id: 3,
      name: r'wordCountChange',
      type: IsarType.long,
    )
  },
  estimateSize: _dailyLogEstimateSize,
  serialize: _dailyLogSerialize,
  deserialize: _dailyLogDeserialize,
  deserializeProp: _dailyLogDeserializeProp,
);

int _dailyLogEstimateSize(
  DailyLog object,
  List<int> offsets,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  var bytesCount = offsets.last;
  {
    final value = object.bookTitle;
    if (value != null) {
      bytesCount += 3 + value.length * 3;
    }
  }
  {
    final value = object.chapterTitle;
    if (value != null) {
      bytesCount += 3 + value.length * 3;
    }
  }
  {
    final value = object.timeStr;
    if (value != null) {
      bytesCount += 3 + value.length * 3;
    }
  }
  return bytesCount;
}

void _dailyLogSerialize(
  DailyLog object,
  IsarWriter writer,
  List<int> offsets,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  writer.writeString(offsets[0], object.bookTitle);
  writer.writeString(offsets[1], object.chapterTitle);
  writer.writeString(offsets[2], object.timeStr);
  writer.writeLong(offsets[3], object.wordCountChange);
}

DailyLog _dailyLogDeserialize(
  Id id,
  IsarReader reader,
  List<int> offsets,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  final object = DailyLog();
  object.bookTitle = reader.readStringOrNull(offsets[0]);
  object.chapterTitle = reader.readStringOrNull(offsets[1]);
  object.timeStr = reader.readStringOrNull(offsets[2]);
  object.wordCountChange = reader.readLong(offsets[3]);
  return object;
}

P _dailyLogDeserializeProp<P>(
  IsarReader reader,
  int propertyId,
  int offset,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  switch (propertyId) {
    case 0:
      return (reader.readStringOrNull(offset)) as P;
    case 1:
      return (reader.readStringOrNull(offset)) as P;
    case 2:
      return (reader.readStringOrNull(offset)) as P;
    case 3:
      return (reader.readLong(offset)) as P;
    default:
      throw IsarError('Unknown property with id $propertyId');
  }
}

import 'package:isar/isar.dart';
import 'outline_node.dart';

part 'outline_tab.g.dart';

enum OutlineType {
  text, // 鏂囨湰妯″紡 (World view, settings)
  list, // 鍒楄〃妯″紡 (Timeline, inventory)
}

@embedded
class OutlineTab {
  String? id;    // 鍞竴鏍囪瘑 (鏆傛椂鐢ㄦ椂闂存埑瀛楃涓?
  String? title; // 椤电鍚嶇О (濡傦細涓栫晫瑙?

  @enumerated
  OutlineType type = OutlineType.text;

  // --- 鏁版嵁瀛樺偍 (浜岄€変竴) ---

  String? textContent; // 鏂囨湰鍨嬫暟鎹?

  List<OutlineNode>? nodes; // 鍒楄〃鍨嬫暟鎹?
}

int _outlineTabEstimateSize(
  OutlineTab object,
  List<int> offsets,
  Map<Type, List<int>> allOffsets,
) {
  var bytesCount = offsets.last;
  {
    final value = object.id;
    if (value != null) {
      bytesCount += 3 + value.length * 3;
    }
  }
  {
    final list = object.nodes;
    if (list != null) {
      bytesCount += 3 + list.length * 3;
      {
        final offsets = allOffsets[OutlineNode]!;
        for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
          final value = list[i];
          bytesCount +=
              OutlineNodeSchema.estimateSize(value, offsets, allOffsets);
        }
      }
    }
  }
  {
    final value = object.textContent;
    if (value != null) {
      bytesCount += 3 + value.length * 3;
    }
  }
  {
    final value = object.title;
    if (value != null) {
      bytesCount += 3 + value.length * 3;
    }
  }
  return bytesCount;
}

}

import 'package:isar/isar.dart';

part 'prompt_template.g.dart';

@collection
class PromptTemplate {
  Id id = Isar.autoIncrement;

  /// 鍦烘櫙浠ｇ爜 (鍞竴鏍囪瘑)
  /// 渚嬪: 'char_extract' (瑙掕壊鎻愬彇), 'char_analysis' (娣卞害鍒嗘瀽)
  @Index(unique: true, replace: true)
  late String sceneCode;

  /// 鏄剧ず鍚嶇О
  late String label;

  // --- 涓夋槑娌荤粨鏋?---

  /// 1. 椤跺眰锛氱郴缁熼璁?(Base Persona)
  /// 渚嬪锛?浣犳槸涓€涓笓涓氱殑灏忚鍔╂墜..."
  String? baseSystemPrompt;

  /// 2. 澶瑰績灞傦細鐢ㄦ埛鑷畾涔夊亸濂?(User Preference) - 绠€鍗曟ā寮忓彧鏀硅繖閲?
  /// 渚嬪锛?璇烽噸鐐瑰叧娉ㄨ鑹茬殑姝﹀櫒鎻忚堪"
  String? userCustomPreference;

  /// 3. 搴曞眰锛氭牸寮忕害鏉?(Format Constraint)
  /// 渚嬪锛?蹇呴』杩斿洖 JSON..."
  String? formatConstraint;

  // --- 楂樼骇妯″紡 ---

  /// 鏄惁寮€鍚珮绾фā寮?(寮€鍚悗蹇界暐涓夋槑娌荤粨鏋勶紝鐩存帴浣跨敤 fullOverride)
  bool isAdvancedMode = false;

  /// 鍏ㄩ噺瑕嗙洊 Prompt (楂樼骇妯″紡涓撶敤)
  String? fullOverride;

  DateTime updatedAt = DateTime.now();
}

    on QueryBuilder<PromptTemplate, PromptTemplate, QSortBy> {

      return query.addSortBy(r'baseSystemPrompt', Sort.asc);
    });

    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'id', Sort.desc);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<PromptTemplate, PromptTemplate, QAfterSortBy>
      thenByIsAdvancedMode() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'isAdvancedMode', Sort.asc);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<PromptTemplate, PromptTemplate, QAfterSortBy>
      thenByIsAdvancedModeDesc() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'isAdvancedMode', Sort.desc);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<PromptTemplate, PromptTemplate, QAfterSortBy> thenByLabel() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'label', Sort.asc);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<PromptTemplate, PromptTemplate, QAfterSortBy> thenByLabelDesc() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'label', Sort.desc);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<PromptTemplate, PromptTemplate, QAfterSortBy> thenBySceneCode() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'sceneCode', Sort.asc);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<PromptTemplate, PromptTemplate, QAfterSortBy>
      thenBySceneCodeDesc() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'sceneCode', Sort.desc);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<PromptTemplate, PromptTemplate, QAfterSortBy> thenByUpdatedAt() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'updatedAt', Sort.asc);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<PromptTemplate, PromptTemplate, QAfterSortBy>
      thenByUpdatedAtDesc() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'updatedAt', Sort.desc);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<PromptTemplate, PromptTemplate, QAfterSortBy>
      thenByUserCustomPreference() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'userCustomPreference', Sort.asc);
    });
  }

  QueryBuilder<PromptTemplate, PromptTemplate, QAfterSortBy>
      thenByUserCustomPreferenceDesc() {
    return QueryBuilder.apply(this, (query) {
      return query.addSortBy(r'userCustomPreference', Sort.desc);
    });
  }
}

    on QueryBuilder<PromptTemplate, PromptTemplate, QDistinct> {

          caseSensitive: caseSensitive);

          caseSensitive: caseSensitive);

      distinctByFullOverride({bool caseSensitive = true}) {

      {bool caseSensitive = true}) {

          caseSensitive: caseSensitive);

--- FILE: lib/pages/image_preview_page.dart ---

import 'dart:io';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import 'package:image_picker/image_picker.dart';
import 'package:image_cropper/image_cropper.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/providers/user_provider.dart';

/// 澶村儚鍏ㄥ睆棰勮椤?
/// 鍔熻兘锛氭煡鐪嬪ぇ鍥俱€佽皟鐢?UserProvider 杩涜澶村儚鏇存崲涓庤鍒?
class ImagePreviewPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const ImagePreviewPage({super.key});
  @override
  State<ImagePreviewPage> createState() => _ImagePreviewPageState();
}

class _ImagePreviewPageState extends State<ImagePreviewPage> {

  Future<void> _pickAndCropImage(BuildContext context) async {
    final picker = ImagePicker();
    final XFile? image = await picker.pickImage(source: ImageSource.gallery);

    if (image == null) return;
    // 1. 瑁佸壀閫昏緫 (1:1 姣斾緥)
    CroppedFile? croppedFile = await ImageCropper().cropImage(
      sourcePath: image.path,
      aspectRatio: const CropAspectRatio(ratioX: 1, ratioY: 1),
      uiSettings: [
        AndroidUiSettings(
          toolbarTitle: '瑁佸垏澶村儚',
          toolbarColor: Colors.black,
          // 銆愬叧閿慨澶嶃€戝叏灞忛瑙堥〉鑳屾櫙鏄粦鑹茬殑锛岀姸鎬佹爮涔熻涓洪粦鑹?
          statusBarColor: Colors.black,
          toolbarWidgetColor: Colors.white,
          activeControlsWidgetColor: Colors.teal,
          initAspectRatio: CropAspectRatioPreset.square,
          lockAspectRatio: true,
        ),
        IOSUiSettings(
          title: '瑁佸垏澶村儚',
          aspectRatioLockEnabled: true,
        ),
      ],
    );
    if (croppedFile != null && context.mounted) {
      // 2. 璋冪敤 Provider 鏇存柊
      try {
        context.read<UserProvider>().updateAvatarPath(croppedFile.path);
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          const SnackBar(content: Text('澶村儚宸叉洿鏂?)),
        );
      } catch (e) {
        debugPrint('Error updating avatar: $e');
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          const SnackBar(content: Text('鏇存柊澶辫触锛岃妫€鏌?UserProvider')),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // 鐩戝惉 UserProvider锛屼互渚垮ご鍍忔洿鏂板悗绔嬪嵆鍒锋柊鏄剧ず
    final userProvider = context.watch<UserProvider>();
    final avatarPath = userProvider.avatarPath;

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: Colors.black,
      // 椤堕儴閫忔槑瀵艰埅鏍?
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
        elevation: 0,
        iconTheme: const IconThemeData(color: Colors.white),
      ),
      // 浣跨敤 Stack 甯冨眬锛屽皢鎸夐挳鎮诞鍦ㄥ浘鐗囦笂鏂?
      body: Stack(
        fit: StackFit.expand,
        children: [
          // 1. 鍥剧墖涓讳綋 (灞呬腑鏄剧ず)
          Center(
            child: Hero(
              tag: 'user_avatar', // 淇濇寔涓?ProfilePage 涓€鑷寸殑 tag 瀹炵幇鍔ㄧ敾琛旀帴
              child: avatarPath != null
                  ? Image.file(
                File(avatarPath),
                fit: BoxFit.contain, // 淇濇寔姣斾緥鏄剧ず瀹屾暣鍥剧墖
              )
                  : const Icon(Icons.account_circle,
                  size: 150, color: Colors.grey),
            ),

          ),

          // 2. 搴曢儴鎿嶄綔鏍?
          Positioned(
            bottom: 60,
            left: 0,
            right: 0,
            child: Center(
              child: ElevatedButton.icon(
                onPressed: () => _pickAndCropImage(context),
                icon: const Icon(Icons.crop_rotate), // 浣跨敤瑁佸垏鍥炬爣鏇寸洿瑙?
                label: const Text('鏇存崲骞惰鍒囧ご鍍?),
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: Colors.white,

                  foregroundColor: Colors.black,
                  padding:
                  const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 32, vertical: 16),
                  shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(30),
                  ),

                ),
              ),
            ),
          )
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'dart:io';
import 'dart:ui';

// 寮曞叆鍚勪釜鍔熻兘椤甸潰

class ProfilePage extends StatefulWidget {
  const ProfilePage({super.key});

  @override
  State<ProfilePage> createState() => _ProfilePageState();
}

class _ProfilePageState extends State<ProfilePage> {

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    // 姣忔杩涘叆椤甸潰鍒锋柊缁熻鏁版嵁锛岀‘淇濇暟瀛楁槸鏈€鏂扮殑
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      context.read<UserProvider>().refreshStats();
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      extendBodyBehindAppBar: true, // 璁╄儗鏅浘寤朵几鍒扮姸鎬佹爮鍚?
      body: ListView(
        padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
        children: [
          // 椤堕儴澶撮儴鍖哄煙 (鑳屾櫙銆佸ご鍍忋€佹暟鎹瑙?
          const _ProfileHeader(),

          const SizedBox(height: 24),

          // 鍔熻兘鍏ュ彛鎸夐挳鏍?
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceEvenly,
            children: [
              _GridItem(
                  icon: Icons.bar_chart,
                  label: '鏁版嵁缁熻',
                  onTap: () {
                    Navigator.push(
                      context,
                      MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const StatsPage()),
                    );
                  }
              ),
              _GridItem(
                  icon: Icons.calendar_month,
                  label: '鐮佸瓧鏃ュ巻',
                  onTap: () {
                    Navigator.push(
                      context,
                      MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const CalendarPage()),
                    );
                  }
              ),
              _GridItem(
                  icon: Icons.delete_outline,
                  label: '鍥炴敹绔?,
                  onTap: () {
                    Navigator.push(
                      context,
                      MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const RecycleBinPage()),
                    );
                  }
              ),
            ],
          ),

          const SizedBox(height: 40),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ProfileHeader extends StatelessWidget {
  const _ProfileHeader();

  // 杈呭姪鏍煎紡鍖栵細灏嗗ぇ鏁板瓧杞负 "1.2涓? 鏍煎紡
  String _formatNumber(int count) {
    if (count < 10000) return count.toString();
    return '${(count / 10000).toStringAsFixed(1)}涓?;
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final user = context.watch<UserProvider>();
    final theme = Theme.of(context);

    // --- 1. 鑳屾櫙閫昏緫鏋勫缓 ---
    Widget backgroundLayer;
    // 浼樺厛浣跨敤鑳屾櫙澶у浘锛屾病鏈夊垯鐢ㄥご鍍忓浘锛屽啀娌℃湁鍒欑敤涓婚鑹?
    final String? bgPath = user.backgroundPath ?? user.avatarPath;

    if (bgPath != null) {
      // 鏈夊浘妯″紡
      backgroundLayer = Stack(
        fit: StackFit.expand,
        children: [
          // 搴曞眰锛氬師鍥?
          Image.file(File(bgPath), fit: BoxFit.cover),

          // 閬僵灞傦細鏍规嵁璁剧疆鍐冲畾鏄€滄ā绯娾€濊繕鏄€滃彉鏆椻€?
          if (user.isBackgroundBlurred)
          // 妯＄硦妯″紡锛氶珮鏂ā绯?+ 娴呴伄缃?
            BackdropFilter(
              filter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 10, sigmaY: 10),
              child: Container(color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.3)),
            )
          else
          // 娓呮櫚妯″紡锛氱函榛戣壊鍗婇€忔槑閬僵 (纭繚鐧借壊鏂囧瓧娓呮櫚)
            Container(color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.4)),
        ],
      );
    } else {
      // 鏃犲浘妯″紡锛氫娇鐢ㄤ富棰樿壊娓愬彉
      final color = theme.colorScheme.primary;
      backgroundLayer = Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          gradient: LinearGradient(
            begin: Alignment.topLeft,
            end: Alignment.bottomRight,
            colors: [color, color.withOpacity(0.6)],
          ),
        ),
      );
    }

    return Container(
      height: 380, // 淇濇寔瓒冲鐨勯珮搴﹀绾充俊鎭?
      clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        // 搴曢儴鍦嗚
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.only(
          bottomLeft: Radius.circular(30),
          bottomRight: Radius.circular(30),
        ),
      ),
      child: Stack(
        children: [
          // A. 鑳屾櫙灞?
          backgroundLayer,

          // B. 鍐呭灞?
          SafeArea(
            bottom: false,
            child: Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24),
              child: Column(
                children: [
                  // 1. 椤堕儴璁剧疆鎸夐挳
                  Align(
                    alignment: Alignment.topRight,
                    child: IconButton(
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.settings, color: Colors.white),
                      onPressed: () {
                        Navigator.push(
                          context,
                          MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const SettingsPage()),
                        );
                      },
                    ),
                  ),

                  const SizedBox(height: 20), // 澧炲姞涓€鐐归《閮ㄨ窛绂?

                  // 2. 鏍稿績鐢ㄦ埛淇℃伅鍖?(Row甯冨眬)
                  Row(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                    children: [
                      // 宸︿晶锛氬ご鍍?
                      GestureDetector(
                        onTap: () {
                          // 鐐瑰嚮澶村儚鍘婚瑙堥〉 (鍙湪棰勮椤垫洿鎹?
                          Navigator.push(
                            context,
                            MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const ImagePreviewPage()),
                          );
                        },
                        child: Hero(
                          tag: 'user_avatar',
                          child: Container(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(2),
                            decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                              color: Colors.white,
                              shape: BoxShape.circle,
                            ),
                            child: CircleAvatar(
                              radius: 42, // 澶村儚绋嶅井澶т竴鐐?
                              backgroundColor: Colors.grey[300],
                              backgroundImage: user.avatarPath != null
                                  ? FileImage(File(user.avatarPath!))
                                  : null,
                              child: user.avatarPath == null
                                  ? const Icon(Icons.person, size: 40, color: Colors.grey)
                                  : null,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),

                      const SizedBox(width: 20), // 澶村儚鍜屾枃瀛楃殑闂磋窛

                      // 鍙充晶锛氫俊鎭爮
                      Expanded(
                        child: Column(
                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, // 宸﹀榻?
                          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                          children: [
                            // 鏄电О + 缂栬緫 (Row)
                            Row(
                              children: [
                                Flexible(
                                  child: Text(
                                    user.nickname,
                                    style: theme.textTheme.headlineSmall?.copyWith(
                                      color: Colors.white,
                                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                      height: 1.2,
                                    ),
                                    maxLines: 1,
                                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                  ),
                                ),
                                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                                GestureDetector(
                                  onTap: () => _showEditNicknameDialog(context, user),
                                  child: const Icon(Icons.edit_square, size: 18, color: Colors.white70),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 8),
                            // 绛夌骇鏍囩 (闈欐€佽楗?
                            Container(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.25),
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                              ),
                              child: const Text(
                                'Lv.1 绛戝熀鏈?,
                                style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),

                  const Spacer(),

                  // 3. 搴曢儴鏁版嵁缁熻 (鏄剧ず鐪熷疄鏁版嵁)
                  Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 40),
                    child: Row(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceAround,
                      children: [
                        _StatItem(
                            value: user.consecutiveDays.toString(),
                            label: '杩炵画鍒涗綔(澶?'
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 20, child: VerticalDivider(color: Colors.white24)),
                        _StatItem(
                            value: _formatNumber(user.totalWords),
                            label: '绱瀛楁暟'
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 20, child: VerticalDivider(color: Colors.white24)),
                        _StatItem(
                            value: user.todayWords.toString(),
                            label: '浠婃棩鐮佸瓧'
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showEditNicknameDialog(BuildContext context, UserProvider user) {
    final controller = TextEditingController(text: user.nickname);
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('淇敼鏄电О'),
        content: TextField(controller: controller, maxLength: 12),
        actions: [
          TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context), child: const Text('鍙栨秷')),
          TextButton(onPressed: () {
            if(controller.text.isNotEmpty) {
              user.updateNickname(controller.text);
              Navigator.pop(context);
            }
          }, child: const Text('淇濆瓨')),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _StatItem extends StatelessWidget {
  final String value;
  final String label;
  const _StatItem({required this.value, required this.label});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
      children: [
        Text(value, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 22, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), // 鏁板瓧绋嶅井鏀惧ぇ
        const SizedBox(height: 4),
        Text(label, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 12)),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _GridItem extends StatelessWidget {
  final IconData icon;
  final String label;
  final VoidCallback onTap;
  const _GridItem({required this.icon, required this.label, required this.onTap});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return InkWell(
      onTap: onTap,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
      child: Container(
        width: 100,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            Icon(icon, size: 30, color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text(label, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13)),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/providers/theme_provider.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/providers/user_provider.dart';
