class SettingsPage extends StatelessWidget {
  const SettingsPage({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final themeProvider = context.watch<ThemeProvider>();
    final userProvider = context.watch<UserProvider>();

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('璁剧疆'),
      ),
      body: ListView(
        children: [
          const SizedBox(height: 10),

          // --- 鍒嗙粍 1: 涓€у寲 ---
          _SectionHeader(title: '涓€у寲'),
          SwitchListTile(
            title: const Text('涓婚〉鑳屾櫙妯＄硦'),
            subtitle: const Text('寮€鍚悗锛屼釜浜轰腑蹇冭儗鏅浘灏嗗簲鐢ㄩ珮鏂ā绯?),
            value: userProvider.isBackgroundBlurred,
            onChanged: (value) {
              userProvider.toggleBackgroundBlur(value);
            },
          ),
          ListTile(
            title: const Text('涓婚棰滆壊'),
            subtitle: Text('褰撳墠鑹插€? #${themeProvider.seedColor.value.toRadixString(16).substring(2).toUpperCase()}'),
            trailing: CircleAvatar(backgroundColor: themeProvider.seedColor, radius: 12),
            onTap: () => _showColorPicker(context, themeProvider),
          ),
          ListTile(
            title: const Text('澶滈棿妯″紡'),
            subtitle: Text(_getThemeModeText(themeProvider.themeMode)),
            trailing: const Icon(Icons.brightness_6),
            onTap: () => _showThemeModeDialog(context, themeProvider),
          ),

          const Divider(),

          // --- 鍒嗙粍 2: AI 涓?鍐欎綔 ---
          _SectionHeader(title: '鏅鸿兘涓庡啓浣?),
          ListTile(
            leading: const Icon(Icons.psychology),
            title: const Text('AI 妯″瀷閰嶇疆'),
            subtitle: const Text('API Key銆佹ā鍨嬮€夋嫨銆佸熀纭€璁惧畾'),
            trailing: const Icon(Icons.chevron_right),
            onTap: () {
              Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const AiConfigPage()));
            },
          ),
          // 銆愬叧閿慨澶嶃€戞仮澶嶁€淎I 鎻愮ず璇嶉厤缃€濆叆鍙?
          ListTile(
            leading: const Icon(Icons.abc, color: Colors.indigo),
            title: const Text('AI 鎻愮ず璇嶉厤缃?),
            subtitle: const Text('鑷畾涔夎鑹叉彁鍙栥€佸ぇ绾茬敓鎴愮瓑 Prompt'),
            trailing: const Icon(Icons.chevron_right),
            onTap: () {
              Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const AiPromptsPage()));
            },
          ),

          const Divider(),

          // --- 鍒嗙粍 3: 鏁版嵁瀹夊叏 ---
          _SectionHeader(title: '鏁版嵁瀹夊叏'),
          ListTile(
            leading: const Icon(Icons.save_as, color: Colors.blue),
            title: const Text('鏁版嵁澶囦唤涓庢仮澶?),
            subtitle: const Text('瀵煎嚭鏁版嵁鍖呫€佽縼绉绘暟鎹埌鏂拌澶?),
            trailing: const Icon(Icons.chevron_right),
            onTap: () {
              Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const DataManagePage()));
            },
          ),

          const Divider(),

          // --- 鍒嗙粍 4: 鍏充簬 ---
          _SectionHeader(title: '鍏充簬'),
          ListTile(
            leading: const Icon(Icons.info_outline),
            title: const Text('鍏充簬钀界瑪'),
            subtitle: const Text('v1.0.4'),
            onTap: () {
              showAboutDialog(
                context: context,
                applicationName: '钀界瑪',
                applicationVersion: '1.0.4',
                applicationLegalese: 'Designed for Writers.',
                applicationIcon: Container(
                  width: 50, height: 50,
                  color: theme.colorScheme.primaryContainer,
                  child: const Icon(Icons.edit_note),
                ),
              );
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  String _getThemeModeText(ThemeMode mode) {
    switch (mode) {
      case ThemeMode.system: return '璺熼殢绯荤粺';
      case ThemeMode.light: return '娴呰壊妯″紡';
      case ThemeMode.dark: return '娣辫壊妯″紡';
    }
  }

  void _showColorPicker(BuildContext context, ThemeProvider provider) {
    final colors = [
      Colors.blue, Colors.purple, Colors.green, Colors.orange,
      Colors.red, Colors.teal, Colors.pink, Colors.indigo
    ];

    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        height: 200,
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            const Text('閫夋嫨涓婚鑹?, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16)),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            Expanded(
              child: GridView.builder(
                gridDelegate: const SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(
                    crossAxisCount: 6, crossAxisSpacing: 10, mainAxisSpacing: 10
                ),
                itemCount: colors.length,
                itemBuilder: (_, i) {
                  final c = colors[i];
                  return GestureDetector(
                    onTap: () {
                      provider.setSeedColor(c);
                      Navigator.pop(ctx);
                    },
                    child: CircleAvatar(backgroundColor: c),
                  );
                },
              ),
            )
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showThemeModeDialog(BuildContext context, ThemeProvider provider) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (_) => SimpleDialog(
        title: const Text('閫夋嫨妯″紡'),
        children: [
          SimpleDialogOption(child: const Text('璺熼殢绯荤粺'), onPressed: () { provider.setThemeMode(ThemeMode.system); Navigator.pop(context); }),
          SimpleDialogOption(child: const Text('娴呰壊妯″紡'), onPressed: () { provider.setThemeMode(ThemeMode.light); Navigator.pop(context); }),
          SimpleDialogOption(child: const Text('娣辫壊妯″紡'), onPressed: () { provider.setThemeMode(ThemeMode.dark); Navigator.pop(context); }),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _SectionHeader extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  const _SectionHeader({required this.title});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 16, 16, 8),
      child: Text(
        title,
        style: TextStyle(
          color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
          fontSize: 14,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:isar/isar.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/models/book.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/services/database_service.dart';

import 'components/empty_view.dart';
import 'components/book_card.dart';
import 'components/hero_book_card.dart';
import 'components/book_edit_dialog.dart'; // 铏界劧鏂囦欢鍚嶆病鍙橈紝浣嗙幇鍦ㄩ噷闈㈡槸 BookEditPanel

class BookshelfPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const BookshelfPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<BookshelfPage> createState() => _BookshelfPageState();
}

class _BookshelfPageState extends State<BookshelfPage> {
  late Stream<List<Book>> _booksStream;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _initBooksStream();
  }

  void _initBooksStream() {
    final isar = DatabaseService().isar;
    _booksStream = isar.books
        .filter()
        .isDeletedEqualTo(false)
        .sortByUpdatedAtDesc()
        .watch(fireImmediately: true);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('鎴戠殑涔︽灦'),
        centerTitle: false,
        actions: [
          IconButton(icon: const Icon(Icons.search), onPressed: () {}),
        ],
      ),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton.extended(
        onPressed: () => _showBookEditPanel(context),
        label: const Text('鏂板缓涔︾睄'),
        icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
      ),
      body: StreamBuilder<List<Book>>(
        stream: _booksStream,
        builder: (context, snapshot) {
          if (snapshot.hasError) return Center(child: Text('Error: ${snapshot.error}'));
          if (!snapshot.hasData) return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());

          final books = snapshot.data!;
          if (books.isEmpty) {
            return EmptyView(onCreate: () => _showBookEditPanel(context));
          }

          return CustomScrollView(
            slivers: [
              // 1. 楂樺厜鍖哄煙 (鏈€杩戠紪杈戠殑涓€鏈功)
              SliverToBoxAdapter(
                child: HeroBookCard(
                  book: books.first,
                ),
              ),

              // 2. 鍒嗗壊鏍囬
              if (books.length > 1)
                SliverToBoxAdapter(
                  child: Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 0, 20, 12),
                    child: Text(
                      '鍏朵粬浣滃搧',
                      style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 14,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.outline
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),

              // 3. 鍓╀綑涔︾睄鍒楄〃
              if (books.length > 1)
                SliverList(
                  delegate: SliverChildBuilderDelegate(
                        (context, index) {
                      final book = books[index + 1];
                      return BookCard(book: book);
                    },
                    childCount: books.length - 1,
                  ),
                ),

              const SliverToBoxAdapter(
                child: SizedBox(height: 80),
              ),
            ],
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  // 銆愬叧閿慨鏀广€戜娇鐢?showModalBottomSheet
  void _showBookEditPanel(BuildContext context) {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true, // 鍏佽楂樺害瓒呰繃鍗婂睆
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent, // 鑳屾櫙閫忔槑锛屾樉绀哄渾瑙?
      builder: (context) => const BookEditPanel(), // 涓嶄紶 book 琛ㄧず鏂板缓
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/models/book.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/pages/writing/writing_page.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/services/export_service.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/pages/bookshelf/components/book_edit_dialog.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/services/database_service.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/widgets/monet_book_cover.dart';

class BookCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final Book book;

  const BookCard({super.key, required this.book});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final dateFormat = DateFormat('yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm');

    return Card(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
      elevation: 0,
      color: theme.colorScheme.surfaceContainerLow,
      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
      child: InkWell(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        onTap: () {
          Navigator.push(
            context,
            MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => WritingPage(
              book: book,
              // 銆愪慨澶嶃€戜紶鍏?-1锛岃〃绀虹敱 WritingPage 鑷姩鍐冲畾璺宠浆鍒颁笂娆￠槄璇荤殑浣嶇疆
              initialChapterIndex: -1,
            )),
          );
        },
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
          child: Row(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              // 灏侀潰
              MonetBookCover(book: book, width: 60, height: 80),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              // 淇℃伅鍖哄煙
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      book.title,
                      style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                      maxLines: 1,
                      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                    Text(
                      '${book.wordCount} 瀛? 鈥? ${dateFormat.format(book.updatedAt)}',
                      style: TextStyle(color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 12),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    Text(
                      book.description?.isNotEmpty == true ? book.description! : '鏆傛棤绠€浠?,
                      style: TextStyle(color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.8), fontSize: 13),
                      maxLines: 2,
                      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              // 鏇村鎿嶄綔鑿滃崟
              PopupMenuButton<String>(
                icon: const Icon(Icons.more_vert),
                onSelected: (value) {
                  if (value == 'edit') _showEditDialog(context);
                  if (value == 'export') _showExportDialog(context);
                  if (value == 'delete') _showDeleteDialog(context);
                },
                itemBuilder: (BuildContext context) => <PopupMenuEntry<String>>[
                  const PopupMenuItem<String>(value: 'edit', child: Text('缂栬緫淇℃伅')),
                  const PopupMenuItem<String>(value: 'export', child: Text('瀵煎嚭')),
                  const PopupMenuItem<String>(value: 'delete', child: Text('鍒犻櫎', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red))),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showEditDialog(BuildContext context) {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      builder: (context) => BookEditPanel(book: book),
    );
  }

  void _showExportDialog(BuildContext context) {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => SafeArea(
        child: Wrap(
          children: [
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.text_snippet_outlined),
              title: const Text('瀵煎嚭涓?TXT'),
              onTap: () { Navigator.pop(ctx); ExportService.exportToTxt(context, book); },
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.book_outlined, color: Colors.green),
              title: const Text('瀵煎嚭涓?EPUB'),
              onTap: () { Navigator.pop(ctx); ExportService.exportToEpub(context, book); },
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showDeleteDialog(BuildContext context) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('纭鍒犻櫎'),
        content: Text('纭畾瑕佸垹闄ゃ€?{book.title}銆嬪悧锛焅n涔︾睄灏嗙Щ鍔ㄥ埌鍥炴敹绔欍€?),
        actions: [
          TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx), child: const Text('鍙栨秷')),
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () async {
              Navigator.pop(ctx);
              final isar = DatabaseService().isar;
              await isar.writeTxn(() async {
                book.isDeleted = true;
                await isar.books.put(book);
              });
            },
            child: const Text('鍒犻櫎', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'dart:io';
import 'dart:ui';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:image_picker/image_picker.dart';
import 'package:image_cropper/image_cropper.dart';
import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/models/book.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/services/database_service.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/widgets/monet_text_field.dart'; // 銆愬紩鍏ユ柊缁勪欢銆?

class BookEditPanel extends StatefulWidget {
  final Book? book;

  const BookEditPanel({super.key, this.book});

  @override
  State<BookEditPanel> createState() => _BookEditPanelState();
}

class _BookEditPanelState extends State<BookEditPanel> {
  final _titleController = TextEditingController();
  final _authorController = TextEditingController();
  final _descController = TextEditingController();

  String? _coverPath;
  int _status = 0; // 0: 杩炶浇涓? 1: 宸插畬缁?

  bool get isEditing => widget.book != null;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    if (isEditing) {
      _titleController.text = widget.book!.title;
      _authorController.text = widget.book!.authorName ?? '';
      _descController.text = widget.book!.description ?? '';
      _coverPath = widget.book!.coverPath;
      _status = widget.book!.status;
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _titleController.dispose();
    _authorController.dispose();
    _descController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final bottomInset = MediaQuery.of(context).viewInsets.bottom;

    return Container(
      height: MediaQuery.of(context).size.height * 0.9,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: theme.colorScheme.surface,
        borderRadius: const BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          _buildHeader(context),
          Expanded(
            child: ListView(
              padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
              children: [
                _buildHeroCover(context),
                Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      // 銆愪娇鐢ㄦ柊缁勪欢銆戞浛鎹簡鍘熸湁鐨?_buildInput
                      MonetTextField(
                        controller: _titleController,
                        label: '涔﹀悕',
                        hint: '璇疯緭鍏ヤ功鍚?,
                        icon: Icons.auto_stories,
                        autoFocus: !isEditing, // 鏂板缓鏃惰嚜鍔ㄨ仛鐒?
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 20),
                      Row(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Expanded(
                            child: MonetTextField(
                              controller: _authorController,
                              label: '浣滆€?,
                              hint: '浣氬悕',
                              icon: Icons.person,
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(width: 16),
                          _buildStatusToggle(theme),
                        ],
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 20),
                      MonetTextField(
                        controller: _descController,
                        label: '绠€浠?,
                        hint: '涓€鍙ヨ瘽浠嬬粛浣犵殑鏁呬簨...',
                        icon: Icons.notes,
                        maxLines: 5,
                        minLines: 3,
                      ),
                      SizedBox(height: bottomInset > 0 ? bottomInset + 20 : 100),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildHeader(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: Colors.grey.withOpacity(0.1))),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            child: const Text('鍙栨秷', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey)),
          ),
          Text(
            isEditing ? '涔︾睄璁剧疆' : '鏂板缓涔︾睄',
            style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
          ),
          FilledButton.tonal(
            onPressed: _save,
            child: const Text('淇濆瓨'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildHeroCover(BuildContext context) {
    final hasCover = _coverPath != null && File(_coverPath!).existsSync();
    final theme = Theme.of(context);

    return SizedBox(
      height: 220,
      child: Stack(
        alignment: Alignment.center,
        children: [
          // 鑳屾櫙妯＄硦灞?
          Positioned.fill(
            child: hasCover
                ? ImageFiltered(
              imageFilter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 30, sigmaY: 30),
              child: Image.file(
                File(_coverPath!),
                fit: BoxFit.cover,
                color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.2),
                colorBlendMode: BlendMode.darken,
              ),
            )
                : Container(
              color: theme.colorScheme.surfaceContainerHighest.withOpacity(0.3),
            ),
          ),

          // 灏侀潰涓讳綋
          GestureDetector(
            onTap: _pickCover,
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                Container(
                  width: 110,
                  height: 146,
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: theme.colorScheme.surface,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                    boxShadow: [
                      BoxShadow(
                        color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.15),
                        blurRadius: 20,
                        offset: const Offset(0, 10),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,
                  child: Stack(
                    fit: StackFit.expand,
                    children: [
                      if (hasCover)
                        Image.file(File(_coverPath!), fit: BoxFit.cover)
                      else
                        Center(
                          child: Icon(Icons.add_photo_alternate_outlined,
                              size: 40, color: theme.colorScheme.primary),
                        ),

                      // 缂栬緫瑙掓爣
                      Positioned(
                        bottom: 0, left: 0, right: 0,
                        child: Container(
                          height: 30,
                          color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.5),
                          alignment: Alignment.center,
                          child: const Text(
                              '鐐瑰嚮鏇存崲',
                              style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 10)
                          ),
                        ),
                      )
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatusToggle(ThemeData theme) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        // 淇濇寔楂樺害涓?Input 瀵归綈
        Container(
          height: 56,
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(4),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: theme.colorScheme.surfaceContainerHighest.withOpacity(0.3),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          ),
          child: Row(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              _buildStatusBtn(0, '杩炶浇', Colors.green),
              _buildStatusBtn(1, '瀹岀粨', Colors.red),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatusBtn(int value, String text, Color activeColor) {
    final isSelected = _status == value;
    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: () => setState(() => _status = value),
      child: AnimatedContainer(
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 200),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 10),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: isSelected ? Colors.white : Colors.transparent,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
          boxShadow: isSelected
              ? [BoxShadow(color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.05), blurRadius: 4)]
              : null,
        ),
        child: Text(
          text,
          style: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 14,
            fontWeight: isSelected ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.normal,
            color: isSelected ? activeColor : Colors.grey,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _pickCover() async {
    final picker = ImagePicker();
    final XFile? image = await picker.pickImage(source: ImageSource.gallery);
    if (image == null) return;

    CroppedFile? croppedFile = await ImageCropper().cropImage(
      sourcePath: image.path,
      aspectRatio: const CropAspectRatio(ratioX: 3, ratioY: 4),
      uiSettings: [
        AndroidUiSettings(
          toolbarTitle: '瑁佸壀灏侀潰',
          toolbarColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surface,
          statusBarColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surface,
          toolbarWidgetColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurface,
          activeControlsWidgetColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
          initAspectRatio: CropAspectRatioPreset.original,
          lockAspectRatio: true,
        ),
        IOSUiSettings(title: '瑁佸壀灏侀潰', aspectRatioLockEnabled: true),
      ],
    );
    if (croppedFile != null) {
      setState(() => _coverPath = croppedFile.path);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _save() async {
    final title = _titleController.text.trim();
    if (title.isEmpty) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('璇疯緭鍏ヤ功鍚?)));
      return;
    }

    final author = _authorController.text.trim().isEmpty ? '浣氬悕' : _authorController.text.trim();
    final desc = _descController.text.trim();

    String? finalCoverPath = _coverPath;
    if (_coverPath != widget.book?.coverPath && _coverPath != null) {
      final directory = await getApplicationDocumentsDirectory();
      final timestamp = DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch;
      final savedImage = await File(_coverPath!).copy('${directory.path}/cover_$timestamp.jpg');
      finalCoverPath = savedImage.path;
    }

    final isar = DatabaseService().isar;
    if (isEditing) {
      await isar.writeTxn(() async {
        final book = widget.book!;
        book.title = title;
        book.authorName = author;
        book.description = desc;
        book.coverPath = finalCoverPath;
        book.status = _status;
        book.updatedAt = DateTime.now();
        await isar.books.put(book);
      });
    } else {
      final newBook = Book()
        ..title = title
        ..authorName = author
        ..description = desc
        ..coverPath = finalCoverPath
        ..status = _status
        ..createdAt = DateTime.now()
        ..updatedAt = DateTime.now();
      await isar.writeTxn(() async {
        await isar.books.put(newBook);
      });
    }
    if (mounted) Navigator.pop(context);
  }
}

class HeroBookCard extends StatefulWidget {

class _HeroBookCardState extends State<HeroBookCard> {
  Color? _themeColor;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _extractColor();
  }

  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(HeroBookCard oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
    if (oldWidget.book.coverPath != widget.book.coverPath) {
      _extractColor();
    }
  }

  Future<void> _extractColor() async {
    if (widget.book.coverPath != null && File(widget.book.coverPath!).existsSync()) {
      try {
        final palette = await PaletteGenerator.fromImageProvider(
          FileImage(File(widget.book.coverPath!)),
          maximumColorCount: 10,
        );
        if (mounted) {
          setState(() {
            _themeColor = palette.dominantColor?.color;
          });
        }
      } catch (e) {
        // ignore
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);

      clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias, // 瑁佸壀鑳屾櫙
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: () {
          Navigator.push(
            context,

              book: widget.book,
              initialChapterIndex: -1,

        child: Stack(

            Positioned.fill(
              child: hasCover
                  ? Image.file(
                File(widget.book.coverPath!),
                fit: BoxFit.cover,
              )
                  : Container(color: theme.colorScheme.primaryContainer),
            ),

            // 2. 妯＄硦婊ら暅 (浠呭綋鏈夊皝闈㈡椂)
            if (hasCover)
              Positioned.fill(
                child: BackdropFilter(
                  filter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 15, sigmaY: 15),
                  child: Container(color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.2)),

              ),

            // 3. 娓愬彉閬僵 (澧炲己鏂囧瓧鍙鎬?
            Positioned.fill(

                    ],

  // ... (淇濈暀鍘熸湰鐨?_showEditPanel 鍜?_showDeleteConfirm 鏂规硶) ...
  void _showEditPanel(BuildContext context) {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => SafeArea(
        child: Wrap(
          children: [
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.edit),
              title: const Text('缂栬緫涔︾睄淇℃伅'),
              onTap: () { Navigator.pop(ctx); showModalBottomSheet(context: context, isScrollControlled: true, backgroundColor: Colors.transparent, builder: (c) => BookEditPanel(book: widget.book)); },
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.text_snippet_outlined),
              title: const Text('瀵煎嚭涓?TXT'),
              onTap: () { Navigator.pop(ctx); ExportService.exportToTxt(context, widget.book); },
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.book_outlined, color: Colors.green),
              title: const Text('瀵煎嚭涓?EPUB'),
              onTap: () { Navigator.pop(ctx); ExportService.exportToEpub(context, widget.book); },
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.delete_outline, color: Colors.red),
              title: const Text('鍒犻櫎涔︾睄', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red)),
              onTap: () { Navigator.pop(ctx); _showDeleteConfirm(context); },
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showDeleteConfirm(BuildContext context) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('纭鍒犻櫎'),
        content: Text('纭畾瑕佸垹闄ゃ€?{widget.book.title}銆嬪悧锛焅n涔︾睄灏嗙Щ鍔ㄥ埌鍥炴敹绔欍€?),
        actions: [
          TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx), child: const Text('鍙栨秷')),
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () async {
              Navigator.pop(ctx);
              final isar = DatabaseService().isar;
              await isar.writeTxn(() async {
                widget.book.isDeleted = true;
                await isar.books.put(widget.book);
              });
            },
            child: const Text('鍒犻櫎', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/services/ai_service.dart';

class AiAssistantSheet extends StatefulWidget {
  final String contextSummary; // 浼犲叆褰撳墠绔犺妭鍐呭浣滀负涓婁笅鏂?
  final Function(String) onResult; // 鍥炶皟锛氭妸 AI 鍐欏ソ鐨勫瓧浼犲洖缂栬緫鍣?

  const AiAssistantSheet({
    super.key,
    required this.contextSummary,
    required this.onResult,
  });

  @override
  State<AiAssistantSheet> createState() => _AiAssistantSheetState();
}

class _AiAssistantSheetState extends State<AiAssistantSheet> {
  final _promptController = TextEditingController();
  bool _isLoading = false;
  String _resultText = '';

  // 蹇嵎鎸囦护
  final List<String> _quickActions = ['缁啓涓€娈?, '娑﹁壊鏂囩瑪', '鐜鎻忓啓', '鍔ㄤ綔鎻忓啓'];

  Future<void> _generate(String action) async {
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
      _resultText = '';
    });

    try {
      final aiProvider = context.read<AiProvider>();

      // 鎴彇鏈熬 800 瀛椾綔涓轰笂涓嬫枃锛岄槻姝?Token 鐖嗙偢
      final contextText = widget.contextSummary.length > 800
          ? widget.contextSummary.substring(widget.contextSummary.length - 800)
          : widget.contextSummary;

      String systemPrompt = '浣犳槸涓€涓笓涓氱殑缃戞枃鍐欎綔鍔╂墜銆傝鏍规嵁鐢ㄦ埛鎻愪緵鐨勪笂涓嬫枃缁啓鎴栨鼎鑹插唴瀹广€傚彧杩斿洖鐢熸垚鐨勫皬璇存鏂囷紝涓嶈鍖呭惈"濂界殑"銆?濡備笅"绛夊簾璇濄€?;
      String userPrompt = '銆愪笂涓嬫枃鐗囨銆戯細\n...$contextText\n\n銆愪换鍔°€戯細$action';

      if (_promptController.text.isNotEmpty) {
        userPrompt += '\n銆愰澶栬姹傘€戯細${_promptController.text}';
      }

      final result = await AiService.generateText(
        aiProvider.config,
        systemPrompt: systemPrompt,
        userPrompt: userPrompt,
      );

      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _resultText = result;
          _isLoading = false;
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _resultText = '鐢熸垚澶辫触: $e';
          _isLoading = false;
        });
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);

    return Container(
      height: MediaQuery.of(context).size.height * 0.75,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: theme.colorScheme.surface,
        borderRadius: const BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(24)),
      ),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Text('AI 鍐欎綔鍔╂墜', style: theme.textTheme.titleLarge),
              IconButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context), icon: const Icon(Icons.close)),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),

          // 蹇嵎鎸囦护
          Wrap(
            spacing: 8, runSpacing: 8,
            children: _quickActions.map((action) => ActionChip(
              label: Text(action),
              onPressed: _isLoading ? null : () => _generate(action),
              avatar: const Icon(Icons.auto_awesome, size: 16),
            )).toList(),
          ),

          const SizedBox(height: 16),

          // 鑷畾涔夎緭鍏?
          TextField(
            controller: _promptController,
            decoration: const InputDecoration(
              labelText: '鑷畾涔夎姹?(鍙€?',
              hintText: '渚嬪锛氬啓寰楁洿鎮蹭激涓€鐐?..',
              border: OutlineInputBorder(),
              isDense: true,
            ),
          ),

          const SizedBox(height: 16),

          // 缁撴灉灞曠ず鍖?
          Expanded(
            child: Container(
              width: double.infinity,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: theme.colorScheme.surfaceContainerLow,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                border: Border.all(color: theme.dividerColor.withOpacity(0.5)),
              ),
              child: _isLoading
                  ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
                  : _resultText.isEmpty
                  ? Center(child: Text('鐐瑰嚮涓婃柟鎸夐挳寮€濮嬬敓鎴?, style: TextStyle(color: theme.colorScheme.outline)))
                  : SingleChildScrollView(child: Text(_resultText, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, height: 1.6))),
            ),
          ),

          const SizedBox(height: 16),

          // 閲囩敤鎸夐挳
          if (_resultText.isNotEmpty && !_isLoading)
            SizedBox(
              width: double.infinity,
              height: 48,
              child: FilledButton.icon(
                onPressed: () {
                  widget.onResult(_resultText); // 浼犲洖缂栬緫鍣?
                  Navigator.pop(context);
                },
                icon: const Icon(Icons.check),
                label: const Text('鎻掑叆鍒板厜鏍囦綅缃?),
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/pages/recycle_bin/recycle_bin_page.dart ---
============================================================
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/models/book.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/services/database_service.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/widgets/monet_book_cover.dart';

class RecycleBinPage extends StatelessWidget {
  const RecycleBinPage({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('鍥炴敹绔?),
        centerTitle: true,
      ),
      body: StreamBuilder<List<Book>>(
        stream: DatabaseService().watchDeletedBooks(),
        builder: (context, snapshot) {
          if (snapshot.hasError) return Center(child: Text('Error: ${snapshot.error}'));
          if (!snapshot.hasData) return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());

          final books = snapshot.data!;

          if (books.isEmpty) {
            return Center(
              child: Column(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                children: [
                  Icon(Icons.delete_outline, size: 80, color: theme.colorScheme.outlineVariant),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Text(
                    '鍥炴敹绔欐槸绌虹殑',
                    style: TextStyle(color: theme.colorScheme.outline, fontSize: 16),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            );
          }

          return ListView.separated(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            itemCount: books.length,
            separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(height: 12),
            itemBuilder: (context, index) {
              final book = books[index];
              return _DeletedBookItem(book: book);
            },
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _DeletedBookItem extends StatelessWidget {
  final Book book;

  const _DeletedBookItem({required this.book});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final dateFormat = DateFormat('yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm');

    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: theme.colorScheme.surfaceContainerLow,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        border: Border.all(color: theme.dividerColor.withOpacity(0.5)),
      ),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          // 灏侀潰 (鍙樼伆浠ョず鍒犻櫎)
          Opacity(
            opacity: 0.6,
            child: MonetBookCover(
              coverPath: book.coverPath,
              title: book.title,
              width: 50,
              height: 70,
              radius: 6,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 16),

          // 淇℃伅
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  book.title,
                  style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16),
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  '鍒犻櫎鏃堕棿: ${dateFormat.format(book.updatedAt)}', // 杩欓噷鏆傛椂鐢?updatedAt 杩戜技浠ｆ浛
                  style: TextStyle(color: theme.colorScheme.outline, fontSize: 12),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  '${book.wordCount} 瀛?,
                  style: TextStyle(color: theme.colorScheme.outline, fontSize: 12),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),

          // 鎿嶄綔鎸夐挳
          Row(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              IconButton.filledTonal(
                onPressed: () => _handleRestore(context),
                icon: const Icon(Icons.restore),
                tooltip: '鎭㈠',
                style: IconButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: Colors.green.withOpacity(0.1),
                  foregroundColor: Colors.green,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              IconButton.filledTonal(
                onPressed: () => _handleDeleteForever(context),
                icon: const Icon(Icons.delete_forever),
                tooltip: '褰诲簳鍒犻櫎',
                style: IconButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: Colors.red.withOpacity(0.1),
                  foregroundColor: Colors.red,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  void _handleRestore(BuildContext context) async {
    await DatabaseService().restoreBook(book.id);
    if (context.mounted) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('涔︾睄宸叉仮澶嶅埌涔︽灦')));
    }
  }

  void _handleDeleteForever(BuildContext context) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('褰诲簳鍒犻櫎'),
        content: const Text('姝ゆ搷浣滃皢姘镐箙鍒犻櫎璇ヤ功鍙婂叾鎵€鏈夌珷鑺傦紝涓旀棤娉曟挙閿€锛乗n\n纭畾瑕佺户缁悧锛?, style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red)),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
            child: const Text('鍙栨秷'),
          ),
          FilledButton(
            style: FilledButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.red),
            onPressed: () async {
              Navigator.pop(ctx); // 鍏冲脊绐?
              await DatabaseService().deleteBookPermanently(book.id);
            },
            child: const Text('褰诲簳绮夌'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

============================================================
--- FILE: lib/pages/settings/ai_config_page.dart ---
============================================================
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/services/ai_service.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/models/ai_config.dart';

class AiConfigPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const AiConfigPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<AiConfigPage> createState() => _AiConfigPageState();
}

class _AiConfigPageState extends State<AiConfigPage> {
  final _keyCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _baseUrlCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _promptCtrl = TextEditingController();

  bool _isTesting = false;
  bool _isFetchingModels = false;
  bool _showAdvanced = false; // 鏄惁灞曞紑楂樼骇璁剧疆

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    final config = context.read<AiProvider>().config;
    _keyCtrl.text = config.apiKey;
    _baseUrlCtrl.text = config.baseUrl;
    _promptCtrl.text = config.customPrompt ?? '';

    // 濡傛灉鏈夐珮绾ц缃唴瀹癸紝榛樿灞曞紑
    if (_baseUrlCtrl.text.isNotEmpty || _promptCtrl.text.isNotEmpty) {
      _showAdvanced = true;
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _keyCtrl.dispose();
    _baseUrlCtrl.dispose();
    _promptCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  // --- 鏍稿績鍔ㄤ綔 1: 娴嬭瘯杩炴帴 (鎭㈠) ---
  Future<void> _testConnection(AiProvider provider) async {
    if (_keyCtrl.text.isEmpty) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('璇峰厛杈撳叆 API Key')));
      return;
    }

    setState(() => _isTesting = true);
    try {
      // 鏋勫缓涓存椂閰嶇疆鐢ㄤ簬娴嬭瘯
      final tempConfig = AiConfig()
        ..provider = provider.config.provider
        ..apiKey = _keyCtrl.text
        ..baseUrl = _baseUrlCtrl.text
        ..modelName = provider.config.modelName
        ..customPrompt = _promptCtrl.text;

      final response = await AiService.generateText(
        tempConfig,
        systemPrompt: 'You are a helpful assistant.',
        userPrompt: 'Reply "Connection OK" if you receive this.',
      );

      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(content: Text('娴嬭瘯鎴愬姛: $response'), backgroundColor: Colors.green),
        );
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) _showErrorDialog('杩炴帴澶辫触', e.toString());
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isTesting = false);
    }
  }

  // --- 鏍稿績鍔ㄤ綔 2: 鎷夊彇妯″瀷鍒楄〃 (鎭㈠) ---
  Future<void> _fetchModelList(AiProvider provider) async {
    if (_keyCtrl.text.isEmpty) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('璇峰厛濉啓 API Key')));
      return;
    }

    setState(() => _isFetchingModels = true);
    try {
      final tempConfig = AiConfig()
        ..provider = provider.config.provider
        ..apiKey = _keyCtrl.text
        ..baseUrl = _baseUrlCtrl.text;

      final models = await AiService.fetchModels(tempConfig);

      if (mounted) {
        provider.setAvailableModels(models);
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(content: Text('鎴愬姛鑾峰彇 ${models.length} 涓ā鍨?), backgroundColor: Colors.green),
        );
        // 濡傛灉褰撳墠妯″瀷涓嶅湪鍒楄〃閲岋紝榛樿閫夌涓€涓?
        if (!models.contains(provider.config.modelName) && models.isNotEmpty) {
          provider.updateConfig(provider.config..modelName = models.first);
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) _showErrorDialog('鑾峰彇妯″瀷鍒楄〃澶辫触', e.toString());
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isFetchingModels = false);
    }
  }

  // 鎵嬪姩杈撳叆妯″瀷瀵硅瘽妗?
  void _showManualModelDialog(BuildContext context, AiProvider provider) {
    final ctrl = TextEditingController(text: provider.config.modelName);
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('鎵嬪姩杈撳叆妯″瀷鍚?),
        content: TextField(
          controller: ctrl,
          decoration: const InputDecoration(hintText: '渚嬪: gpt-4o, deepseek-chat'),
          autofocus: true,
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx), child: const Text('鍙栨秷')),
          FilledButton(
            onPressed: () {
              if (ctrl.text.isNotEmpty) {
                // 鏇存柊妯″瀷鍚?
                final newConfig = provider.config..modelName = ctrl.text.trim();
                provider.updateConfig(newConfig);
                Navigator.pop(ctx);
              }
            },
            child: const Text('纭畾'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showErrorDialog(String title, String msg) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        title: Text(title, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.red)),
        content: SingleChildScrollView(child: Text(msg)),
        actions: [TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx), child: const Text('鍏抽棴'))],
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final provider = context.watch<AiProvider>();
    final config = provider.config;

    // 鍑嗗涓嬫媺鍒楄〃鏁版嵁
    final modelList = provider.availableModels;
    final dropdownItems = List<String>.from(modelList);
    // 纭繚褰撳墠閫変腑鐨勬ā鍨嬪湪鍒楄〃閲岋紝鍚﹀垯鍔犺繘鍘伙紙閬垮厤 Dropdown 鎶ラ敊锛?
    if (config.modelName.isNotEmpty && !dropdownItems.contains(config.modelName)) {
      dropdownItems.insert(0, config.modelName);
    }

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('AI 妯″瀷閰嶇疆')),
      body: ListView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        children: [
          // 1. 鍘傚晢閫夋嫨
          DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
            decoration: const InputDecoration(
              labelText: 'AI 鏈嶅姟鍟?,
              border: OutlineInputBorder(),
              prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.cloud_outlined),
            ),
            value: config.provider,
            items: const [
              DropdownMenuItem(value: 'google', child: Text('Google Gemini')),
              DropdownMenuItem(value: 'openai', child: Text('OpenAI / 鍏煎鎺ュ彛')),
              DropdownMenuItem(value: 'silicon', child: Text('纭呭熀娴佸姩 (SiliconFlow)')),
              DropdownMenuItem(value: 'deepseek', child: Text('DeepSeek 瀹樻柟')),
              DropdownMenuItem(value: 'custom', child: Text('鑷畾涔?(OpenAI 鏍煎紡)')),
            ],
            onChanged: (val) {
              if (val != null) {
                // 鍒囨崲鍘傚晢
                final newConfig = config..provider = val;
                provider.updateConfig(newConfig);

                // 鑷姩濉厖 BaseURL (浠呭綋杈撳叆妗嗕负绌烘椂锛屾垨鑰呯敤鎴峰垰鍒囨崲浜嗗巶鍟?
                if (val == 'silicon') {
                  _baseUrlCtrl.text = 'https://api.siliconflow.cn/v1';
                } else if (val == 'deepseek') {
                  _baseUrlCtrl.text = 'https://api.deepseek.com';
                } else if (val == 'google') {
                  _baseUrlCtrl.text = '';
                }
                // 鍚屾鏇存柊 config 涓殑 baseUrl
                provider.updateConfig(config..baseUrl = _baseUrlCtrl.text);
              }
            },
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),

          // 2. API Key
          TextField(
            controller: _keyCtrl,
            decoration: const InputDecoration(
              labelText: 'API Key',
              hintText: 'sk-...',
              border: OutlineInputBorder(),
              prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.vpn_key),
            ),
            obscureText: true,
            onChanged: (val) {
              // 瀹炴椂淇濆瓨
              provider.updateConfig(config..apiKey = val);
            },
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),

          // 3. 妯″瀷閫夋嫨 (甯﹀埛鏂版寜閽殑缁忓吀甯冨眬)
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                  isExpanded: true,
                  decoration: const InputDecoration(
                    labelText: '妯″瀷鍚嶇О',
                    border: OutlineInputBorder(),
                    prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.model_training),
                  ),
                  value: dropdownItems.isEmpty ? null : config.modelName,
                  items: dropdownItems.map((m) {
                    return DropdownMenuItem(value: m, child: Text(m, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis));
                  }).toList(),
                  onChanged: (val) {
                    if (val != null) {
                      provider.updateConfig(config..modelName = val);
                    }
                  },
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              // 銆愭牳蹇冨洖褰掋€戝埛鏂版寜閽?
              IconButton.filledTonal(
                onPressed: _isFetchingModels ? null : () => _fetchModelList(provider),
                icon: _isFetchingModels
                    ? const SizedBox(width: 20, height: 20, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2))
                    : const Icon(Icons.refresh),
                tooltip: '浠庢湇鍔″櫒鑾峰彇鏈€鏂版ā鍨嬪垪琛?,
              ),
            ],
          ),

          // 4. 鎵嬪姩杈撳叆鍏ュ彛 (缁忓吀甯冨眬)
          Align(
            alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
            child: TextButton.icon(
              onPressed: () {
                _showManualModelDialog(context, provider);
              },
              icon: const Icon(Icons.edit, size: 14),
              label: const Text('鎵嬪姩杈撳叆妯″瀷鍚?, style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12)),
            ),
          ),

          const Divider(height: 32),

          // 5. 楂樼骇璁剧疆 (BaseURL + Prompt)
          ListTile(
            title: const Text('楂樼骇璁剧疆'),
            subtitle: const Text('Base URL銆佺郴缁熸彁绀鸿瘝'),
            trailing: Icon(_showAdvanced ? Icons.expand_less : Icons.expand_more),
            onTap: () => setState(() => _showAdvanced = !_showAdvanced),
          ),

          if (_showAdvanced || config.provider == 'custom') ...[
            TextField(
              controller: _baseUrlCtrl,
              decoration: InputDecoration(
                labelText: 'Base URL (API 鍦板潃)',
                hintText: 'https://api.openai.com/v1',
                border: const OutlineInputBorder(),
                prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.link),
                helperText: config.provider != 'custom' ? '閫氬父涓嶉渶瑕佷慨鏀癸紝闄ら潪浣跨敤浠ｇ悊' : '蹇呭～',
              ),
              onChanged: (val) => provider.updateConfig(config..baseUrl = val),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            TextField(
              controller: _promptCtrl,
              maxLines: 3,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(
                labelText: '鑷畾涔夌郴缁熸彁绀鸿瘝 (System Prompt)',
                hintText: '瑕嗙洊榛樿 AI 浜鸿锛屼緥濡傦細浣犳槸涓€涓弗璋ㄧ殑绉戝够灏忚瀹?..',
                border: OutlineInputBorder(),
              ),
              onChanged: (val) => provider.updateConfig(config..customPrompt = val),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
          ],

          const SizedBox(height: 24),

          // 6. 娴嬭瘯杩炴帴鎸夐挳 (缁忓吀鍥炲綊)
          SizedBox(
            height: 50,
            child: FilledButton.icon(
              onPressed: _isTesting ? null : () => _testConnection(provider),
              icon: const Icon(Icons.network_check),
              label: Text(_isTesting ? '娴嬭瘯杩炴帴涓?..' : '娴嬭瘯杩炴帴 (淇濆瓨骞堕獙璇?'),
            ),
          ),

          const SizedBox(height: 40),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/models/prompt_template.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/services/prompt_manager.dart';

class AiPromptsPage extends StatelessWidget {
  const AiPromptsPage({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('AI 鎻愮ず璇嶉厤缃?)),
      body: ListView(
        children: [
          _buildSectionHeader(context, '瑙掕壊鐩稿叧'),
          _buildItem(
            context,
            PromptManager.SCENE_CHAR_ANALYSIS,
            '瑙掕壊娣卞害鍒嗘瀽',
            '鐢ㄤ簬瑙掕壊璇︽儏椤电殑鏃堕棿杞翠笌鐢熷钩鍚屾',
            Icons.person_search,
          ),
          _buildItem(
            context,
            PromptManager.SCENE_CHAR_Extract,
            '绔犺妭瑙掕壊鎻愬彇',
            '鐢ㄤ簬瑙掕壊鍒楄〃椤电殑鎵归噺鎻愬彇鍔熻兘',
            Icons.people_alt,
          ),
          const Divider(),
          _buildSectionHeader(context, '澶х翰鐩稿叧'),
          _buildItem(
            context,
            PromptManager.SCENE_OUTLINE_NODE,
            '绔犺妭缁嗙翰鐢熸垚',
            '鏍规嵁褰撳墠绔犺妭鐢熸垚鍓ф儏鑺傜偣',
            Icons.segment,
          ),
          _buildItem(
            context,
            PromptManager.SCENE_WORLD_SETTING,
            '璁惧畾/涓栫晫瑙傝ˉ鍏?,
            '鏍规嵁姝ｆ枃鑷姩琛ュ厖璁惧畾鏂囨。',
            Icons.auto_fix_high,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSectionHeader(BuildContext context, String title) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 16, 16, 8),
      child: Text(title, style: TextStyle(color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildItem(BuildContext context, String code, String title, String subtitle, IconData icon) {
    return ListTile(
      leading: Icon(icon, color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant),
      title: Text(title),
      subtitle: Text(subtitle, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12)),
      trailing: const Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_ios, size: 16),
      onTap: () {
        Navigator.push(
          context,
          MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => PromptEditorPage(sceneCode: code, title: title)),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

class PromptEditorPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String sceneCode;
  final String title;

  const PromptEditorPage({super.key, required this.sceneCode, required this.title});

  @override
  State<PromptEditorPage> createState() => _PromptEditorPageState();
}

class _PromptEditorPageState extends State<PromptEditorPage> {
  PromptTemplate? _template;
  bool _isLoading = true;

  final _simpleCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _advancedCtrl = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadData();
  }

  Future<void> _loadData() async {
    final t = await PromptManager.getTemplate(widget.sceneCode);
    setState(() {
      _template = t;
      _simpleCtrl.text = t.userCustomPreference ?? '';
      _advancedCtrl.text = t.fullOverride ?? '';
      _isLoading = false;
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _simpleCtrl.dispose();
    _advancedCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (_isLoading) return const Scaffold(body: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()));

    final isAdvanced = _template!.isAdvancedMode;

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(widget.title),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.save),
            onPressed: () => _save(context),
          )
        ],
      ),
      body: ListView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        children: [
          SwitchListTile(
            title: const Text('楂樼骇寮€鍙戣€呮ā寮?),
            subtitle: const Text('寮€鍚悗鍙紪杈戝畬鏁寸殑 System Prompt锛屼絾闇€鑷淇濊瘉 JSON 鏍煎紡姝ｇ‘銆?),
            value: isAdvanced,
            onChanged: (val) async {
              await PromptManager.toggleAdvancedMode(widget.sceneCode, val);
              _loadData();
            },
          ),
          const Divider(),

          if (!isAdvanced) ...[
            _buildSectionTitle('鑷畾涔夊亸濂?(绠€鍗曟ā寮?'),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            const Text('鍦ㄦ杈撳叆鎮ㄥ笇鏈?AI 娉ㄦ剰鐨勭粏鑺傘€侫I 浼氳嚜鍔ㄥ皢鍏舵彃鍏ュ埌鏍囧噯鎸囦护涓€?, style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey, fontSize: 12)),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            TextField(
              controller: _simpleCtrl,
              maxLines: 5,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(
                border: OutlineInputBorder(),
                hintText: '渚嬪锛氳閲嶇偣鍏虫敞鍓ф儏杞姌锛屽拷鐣ョ幆澧冩弿鍐?..',
                filled: true,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            ExpansionTile(
              title: const Text('鏌ョ湅瀹屾暣鎸囦护棰勮', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14)),
              children: [
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                  color: Colors.grey.withOpacity(0.1),
                  width: double.infinity,
                  child: Text(
                    "${_template!.baseSystemPrompt}\n\n[鐢ㄦ埛鍋忓ソ]: ${_simpleCtrl.text}\n\n${_template!.formatConstraint}",
                    style: const TextStyle(fontFamily: 'monospace', fontSize: 12),
                  ),
                )
              ],
            )
          ] else ...[
            _buildSectionTitle('瀹屾暣 System Prompt (楂樼骇妯″紡)'),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.orange.withOpacity(0.1), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
              child: const Row(
                children: [
                  Icon(Icons.warning_amber, color: Colors.orange, size: 20),
                  SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Expanded(child: Text('璀﹀憡锛氬鏋滆鍦烘櫙闇€瑕佽繑鍥?JSON锛岃鍔″繀鍦ㄦ寚浠や腑淇濈暀 JSON 鏍煎紡瀹氫箟锛?, style: TextStyle(color: Colors.orange, fontSize: 12))),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            TextField(
              controller: _advancedCtrl,
              maxLines: null,
              minLines: 10,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(
                border: OutlineInputBorder(),
                hintText: '杈撳叆瀹屾暣鐨?System Prompt...',
                filled: true,
              ),
              style: const TextStyle(fontFamily: 'monospace', fontSize: 13),
            ),
          ]
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSectionTitle(String title) {
    return Text(
      title,
      style: TextStyle(
        color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _save(BuildContext context) async {
    if (_template!.isAdvancedMode) {
      await PromptManager.updateFullOverride(widget.sceneCode, _advancedCtrl.text);
    } else {
      await PromptManager.updateUserPreference(widget.sceneCode, _simpleCtrl.text);
    }
    if (context.mounted) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('淇濆瓨鎴愬姛')));
      Navigator.pop(context);
    }
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/pages/settings/ai_settings_page.dart ---

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/services/ai_service.dart';
// 銆愬叧閿慨澶嶃€戝紩鍏ユā鍨嬪畾涔?
import 'package:monet_writer/models/ai_config.dart';

class AiSettingsPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const AiSettingsPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<AiSettingsPage> createState() => _AiSettingsPageState();
}

class _AiSettingsPageState extends State<AiSettingsPage> {
  final _keyCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _baseUrlCtrl = TextEditingController();

  bool _isTesting = false;
  bool _isFetchingModels = false;
  bool _showAdvanced = false; // 鏄惁灞曞紑楂樼骇璁剧疆(Base URL)

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    final config = context.read<AiProvider>().config;
    _keyCtrl.text = config.apiKey;
    _baseUrlCtrl.text = config.baseUrl ?? '';
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _keyCtrl.dispose();
    _baseUrlCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  // 娴嬭瘯鐢熸垚
  Future<void> _testConnection(AiProvider provider) async {
    setState(() => _isTesting = true);
    try {
      final response = await AiService.generateText(
        provider.config,
        systemPrompt: 'You are a helpful assistant.',
        userPrompt: 'Reply "Connection OK" if you receive this.',
      );

      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(content: Text('鎴愬姛: $response'), backgroundColor: Colors.green),
        );
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) _showErrorDialog(e.toString());
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isTesting = false);
    }
  }

  // 鍔ㄦ€佹媺鍙栨ā鍨嬪垪琛?
  Future<void> _fetchModelList(AiProvider provider) async {
    if (_keyCtrl.text.isEmpty) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('璇峰厛濉啓 API Key')));
      return;
    }

    setState(() => _isFetchingModels = true);
    try {
      // 涓存椂鏋勫缓涓€涓?config 瀵硅薄鐢ㄤ簬璇锋眰
      final tempConfig = AiConfig(
        provider: provider.config.provider,
        apiKey: _keyCtrl.text,
        baseUrl: _baseUrlCtrl.text.isEmpty ? null : _baseUrlCtrl.text,
      );

      final models = await AiService.fetchModels(tempConfig);

      if (mounted) {
        await provider.updateModelList(models);
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(content: Text('鎴愬姛鑾峰彇 ${models.length} 涓ā鍨?), backgroundColor: Colors.green),
        );
        // 濡傛灉褰撳墠妯″瀷涓嶅湪鍒楄〃閲岋紝榛樿閫夌涓€涓?
        if (!models.contains(provider.config.modelName) && models.isNotEmpty) {
          provider.updateConfig(modelName: models.first);
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) _showErrorDialog('鑾峰彇妯″瀷鍒楄〃澶辫触: $e');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isFetchingModels = false);
    }
  }

  void _showErrorDialog(String msg) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('鍑洪敊'),
        content: SingleChildScrollView(child: Text(msg)),
        actions: [TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx), child: const Text('鍏抽棴'))],
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final provider = context.watch<AiProvider>();
    final config = provider.config;
    final modelList = provider.currentModelList;

    // 纭繚褰撳墠閫変腑鐨勬ā鍨嬪湪鍒楄〃閲岋紝鍚﹀垯鍔犺繘鍘伙紙閬垮厤 Dropdown 鎶ラ敊锛?
    final dropdownItems = List<String>.from(modelList);
    if (config.modelName.isNotEmpty && !dropdownItems.contains(config.modelName)) {
      dropdownItems.insert(0, config.modelName);
    }

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('AI 妯″瀷閰嶇疆')),
      body: ListView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        children: [
          // 1. 鍘傚晢閫夋嫨
          DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
            decoration: const InputDecoration(
              labelText: 'AI 鏈嶅姟鍟?,
              border: OutlineInputBorder(),
              prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.cloud_outlined),
            ),
            value: config.provider,
            items: const [
              DropdownMenuItem(value: 'google', child: Text('Google Gemini')),
              DropdownMenuItem(value: 'openai', child: Text('OpenAI')),
              DropdownMenuItem(value: 'siliconflow', child: Text('纭呭熀娴佸姩 (SiliconFlow)')),
              DropdownMenuItem(value: 'deepseek', child: Text('DeepSeek 瀹樻柟')),
              DropdownMenuItem(value: 'custom', child: Text('鑷畾涔?(OpenAI 鏍煎紡)')),
            ],
            onChanged: (val) {
              if (val != null) {
                provider.updateConfig(provider: val);
                // 鍒囨崲鍘傚晢鍚庯紝BaseURL 涔熶細鍙橈紝鍒锋柊杈撳叆妗?(浠?Model 鑾峰彇)
                _baseUrlCtrl.text = provider.config.baseUrl ?? '';
              }
            },
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),

          // 2. API Key
          TextField(
            controller: _keyCtrl,
            decoration: const InputDecoration(
              labelText: 'API Key',
              hintText: 'sk-...',
              border: OutlineInputBorder(),
              prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.vpn_key),
            ),
            obscureText: true,
            onChanged: (val) => provider.updateConfig(apiKey: val),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),

          // 3. 妯″瀷閫夋嫨 (甯﹀埛鏂版寜閽?
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                  isExpanded: true,
                  decoration: const InputDecoration(
                    labelText: '妯″瀷鍚嶇О',
                    border: OutlineInputBorder(),
                    prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.model_training),
                  ),
                  value: dropdownItems.isEmpty ? null : config.modelName,
                  items: dropdownItems.map((m) {
                    return DropdownMenuItem(value: m, child: Text(m, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis));
                  }).toList(),
                  onChanged: (val) {
                    if (val != null) provider.updateConfig(modelName: val);
                  },
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              IconButton.filledTonal(
                onPressed: _isFetchingModels ? null : () => _fetchModelList(provider),
                icon: _isFetchingModels
                    ? const SizedBox(width: 20, height: 20, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2))
                    : const Icon(Icons.refresh),
                tooltip: '浠庢湇鍔″櫒鑾峰彇鏈€鏂版ā鍨嬪垪琛?,
              ),
            ],
          ),

          // 4. 鎵嬪姩杈撳叆妯″瀷鍏ュ彛 (鍏滃簳)
          Align(
            alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
            child: TextButton.icon(
              onPressed: () {
                _showManualModelDialog(context, provider);
              },
              icon: const Icon(Icons.edit, size: 14),
              label: const Text('鎵嬪姩杈撳叆妯″瀷鍚?, style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12)),
            ),
          ),

          const Divider(height: 32),

          // 5. 楂樼骇璁剧疆 (Base URL)
          ListTile(
            title: const Text('楂樼骇璁剧疆'),
            trailing: Icon(_showAdvanced ? Icons.expand_less : Icons.expand_more),
            onTap: () => setState(() => _showAdvanced = !_showAdvanced),
          ),

          if (_showAdvanced || config.provider == 'custom')
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 16),
              child: TextField(
                controller: _baseUrlCtrl,
                decoration: InputDecoration(
                  labelText: 'Base URL (API 鍦板潃)',
                  hintText: 'https://api.openai.com/v1',
                  border: const OutlineInputBorder(),
                  prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.link),
                  helperText: config.provider != 'custom' ? '閫氬父涓嶉渶瑕佷慨鏀癸紝闄ら潪浣跨敤浠ｇ悊' : '蹇呭～',
                ),
                onChanged: (val) => provider.updateConfig(baseUrl: val),
              ),
            ),

          const SizedBox(height: 24),

          // 6. 娴嬭瘯杩炴帴
          SizedBox(
            height: 50,
            child: FilledButton.icon(
              onPressed: _isTesting ? null : () => _testConnection(provider),
              icon: const Icon(Icons.network_check),
              label: Text(_isTesting ? '娴嬭瘯杩炴帴涓?..' : '娴嬭瘯杩炴帴'),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showManualModelDialog(BuildContext context, AiProvider provider) {
    final ctrl = TextEditingController(text: provider.config.modelName);
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('鎵嬪姩杈撳叆妯″瀷鍚?),
        content: TextField(
          controller: ctrl,
          decoration: const InputDecoration(hintText: '渚嬪: gpt-4o'),
          autofocus: true,
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx), child: const Text('鍙栨秷')),
          FilledButton(
            onPressed: () {
              if (ctrl.text.isNotEmpty) {
                provider.updateConfig(modelName: ctrl.text);
                Navigator.pop(ctx);
              }
            },
            child: const Text('纭畾'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/services/backup_service.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/providers/user_provider.dart';

class DataManagePage extends StatefulWidget {
  const DataManagePage({super.key});

  @override
  State<DataManagePage> createState() => _DataManagePageState();
}

class _DataManagePageState extends State<DataManagePage> {
  bool _isLoading = false;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('鏁版嵁绠＄悊'),
      ),
      body: Stack(
        children: [
          ListView(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            children: [
              // 椤堕儴鎻愮ず鍗＄墖
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: theme.colorScheme.primaryContainer.withOpacity(0.5),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Icon(Icons.info_outline, color: theme.colorScheme.primary),
                    const SizedBox(width: 12),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        '璇峰畾鏈熷浠芥偍鐨勬暟鎹€傚浠芥枃浠跺寘鍚墍鏈変功绫嶃€佺珷鑺傘€佽瀹氬強鍥剧墖璧勬簮銆?,
                        style: TextStyle(color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize: 13),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 24),

              // 澶囦唤鎸夐挳
              _buildActionCard(
                context,
                title: '鍏ㄩ噺澶囦唤 (瀵煎嚭)',
                subtitle: '鐢熸垚 .zip 鏍煎紡鐨勫浠芥枃浠讹紝鏀寔鍒嗕韩鎴栦繚瀛樺埌鏈湴銆?,
                icon: Icons.upload_file,
                color: Colors.blue,
                onTap: _handleBackup,
              ),

              const SizedBox(height: 16),

              // 鎭㈠鎸夐挳
              _buildActionCard(
                context,
                title: '鏁版嵁鎭㈠ (瀵煎叆)',
                subtitle: '浠庡浠芥枃浠舵仮澶嶆暟鎹€傝鍛婏細杩欏皢瑕嗙洊褰撳墠鐨勫叏閮ㄦ暟鎹紒',
                icon: Icons.settings_backup_restore,
                color: Colors.red, // 绾㈣壊璀︾ず
                onTap: _handleRestore,
              ),
            ],
          ),

          // Loading 閬僵
          if (_isLoading)
            Container(
              color: Colors.black45,
              child: const Center(
                child: Column(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.white),
                    SizedBox(height: 16),
                    Text('姝ｅ湪澶勭悊鏁版嵁...', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildActionCard(
      BuildContext context, {
        required String title,
        required String subtitle,
        required IconData icon,
        required Color color,
        required VoidCallback onTap,
      }) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    return Card(
      elevation: 0,
      color: theme.colorScheme.surfaceContainerLow,
      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        side: BorderSide(color: theme.dividerColor.withOpacity(0.2)),
      ),
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: onTap,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: color.withOpacity(0.1),
                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                ),
                child: Icon(icon, color: color, size: 28),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(title, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                    Text(subtitle, style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: theme.colorScheme.outline)),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              const Icon(Icons.chevron_right, color: Colors.grey),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // 澶勭悊澶囦唤
  Future<void> _handleBackup() async {
    setState(() => _isLoading = true);
    // 绋嶅井寤惰繜涓€涓嬭 Loading 鏄剧ず鍑烘潵
    await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 300));

    if (!mounted) return;

    try {
      await BackupService().createBackup(context);
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoading = false);
    }
  }

  // 澶勭悊鎭㈠
  Future<void> _handleRestore() async {
    // 1. 寮瑰嚭璀﹀憡纭妗?
    final confirm = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('鈿狅笍 纭鎭㈠鏁版嵁锛?),
        content: const Text(
          '姝ゆ搷浣滃皢銆愬交搴曟竻绌恒€戝綋鍓?App 鍐呯殑鎵€鏈変功绫嶅拰璁剧疆锛屽苟鏇挎崲涓哄浠芥枃浠朵腑鐨勫唴瀹广€俓n\n璇ユ搷浣滀笉鍙挙閿€锛?,
          style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, false), child: const Text('鍙栨秷')),
          FilledButton(
            style: FilledButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.red),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, true),
            child: const Text('纭畾瑕嗙洊'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );

    if (confirm != true) return;

    setState(() => _isLoading = true);
    await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 300));

    if (!mounted) return;

    try {
      // 2. 璋冪敤鎭㈠鏈嶅姟
      final success = await BackupService().restoreBackup(context);

      if (success) {
        // 3. 鎭㈠鎴愬姛鍚庯紝蹇呴』鍒锋柊鍐呭瓨涓殑鐘舵€?
        if (mounted) {
          await context.read<UserProvider>().loadUserData(); // 鍒锋柊澶村儚銆佽缃?
          await context.read<UserProvider>().refreshStats(); // 鍒锋柊缁熻

          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
            const SnackBar(
              content: Text('鉁?鏁版嵁鎭㈠鎴愬姛锛?),
              backgroundColor: Colors.green,
              duration: Duration(seconds: 2),
            ),
          );

          // 鍙€夛細杩斿洖涓婁竴椤垫垨閲嶅惎 App
          // Navigator.pop(context);
        }
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoading = false);
    }
  }
}
