--- FILE: lib/services/database_service.dart ---
============================================================
import 'package:isar/isar.dart';
import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/models/book.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/models/chapter.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/models/prompt_template.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/models/daily_stats.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/models/ai_config.dart';

// 寮曞叆鏂扮殑 Repositories
import 'package:monet_writer/repositories/book_repository.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/repositories/chapter_repository.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/repositories/stats_repository.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/repositories/ai_config_repository.dart';

class DatabaseService {
  static final DatabaseService _instance = DatabaseService._internal();
  factory DatabaseService() => _instance;
  DatabaseService._internal();

  late Isar _isar;
  Isar get isar => _isar;

  // 鎸佹湁 Repositories
  late BookRepository bookRepo;
  late ChapterRepository chapterRepo;
  late StatsRepository statsRepo;
  late AiConfigRepository aiConfigRepo;

  /// 鍒濆鍖栨暟鎹簱
  Future<void> init() async {
    final dir = await getApplicationDocumentsDirectory();
    _isar = await Isar.open(
      [
        BookSchema,
        ChapterSchema,
        PromptTemplateSchema,
        DailyStatsSchema,
        AiConfigSchema,
      ],
      directory: dir.path,
    );

    // 鍒濆鍖?Repositories
    bookRepo = BookRepository(_isar);
    chapterRepo = ChapterRepository(_isar);
    statsRepo = StatsRepository(_isar);
    aiConfigRepo = AiConfigRepository(_isar);
  }

  // ==================== 妗ユ帴灞?(Bridge Layer) ====================
  // 涓嬮潰鐨勬柟娉曟槸涓轰簡淇濇寔瀵规棫浠ｇ爜鐨勫吋瀹规€с€?
  // 瀹為檯涓婂畠浠彧鏄妸浠诲姟濮旀墭缁欎簡 Repository銆?

  // --- 缁熻鐩稿叧 ---
  Future<void> updateDailyStats(int delta, String bookTitle, String chapterTitle) =>
      statsRepo.updateDailyStats(delta, bookTitle, chapterTitle);

  Future<int> getTodayWordCount() => statsRepo.getTodayWordCount();
  Future<int> getTotalWordCount() => statsRepo.getTotalWordCount();
  Future<int> getConsecutiveDays() => statsRepo.getConsecutiveDays();
  Future<List<DailyStats>> getWeeklyStats() => statsRepo.getWeeklyStats();
  Future<List<DailyStats>> getMonthlyStats(DateTime month) => statsRepo.getMonthlyStats(month);
  Future<List<DailyStats>> getCurrentMonthStats() => statsRepo.getMonthlyStats(DateTime.now());
  Future<int> getActiveDaysCount() async => await _isar.dailyStats.filter().wordCountGreaterThan(0).count(); // 绠€鍗曟煡璇繚鐣欐垨绉诲叆Repo鐨嗗彲
  Future<DailyStats?> getPeakRecord() async => await _isar.dailyStats.where().sortByWordCountDesc().findFirst();

  // --- 涔︾睄鐩稿叧 ---
  Future<void> createBook(String title, {String? desc, String? coverPath}) =>
      bookRepo.createBook(title, desc: desc, coverPath: coverPath);

  Future<List<Book>> getAllBooks() => bookRepo.getAllBooks();
  Stream<List<Book>> watchDeletedBooks() => bookRepo.watchDeletedBooks();
  Future<void> restoreBook(int bookId) => bookRepo.restoreBook(bookId);
  Future<void> deleteBookPermanently(int bookId) => bookRepo.deleteBookPermanently(bookId);

  // --- 绔犺妭鐩稿叧 ---
  Future<List<Chapter>> getChaptersForBook(int bookId) => chapterRepo.getChaptersForBook(bookId);
  Future<void> createChapter(int bookId, String title) => chapterRepo.createChapter(bookId, title);
  Future<void> updateChapterContent(int chapterId, String content, int wordCount) =>
      chapterRepo.updateChapterContent(chapterId, content, wordCount);

  // --- AI 閰嶇疆鐩稿叧 ---
  Future<List<AiConfig>> getAllAiConfigs() => aiConfigRepo.getAllConfigs();
  Future<void> saveAiConfig(AiConfig config) => aiConfigRepo.saveConfig(config);
  Future<void> deleteAiConfig(int id) => aiConfigRepo.deleteConfig(id);
}

import 'dart:io';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:isar/isar.dart';
import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';
import 'package:flutter_file_dialog/flutter_file_dialog.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/models/book.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/models/chapter.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/services/database_service.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/utils/epub_builder.dart'; // 銆愬紩鍏?EpubBuilder銆?

class ExportService {

  // 1. TXT 瀵煎嚭閫昏緫 (淇濇寔涓嶅彉)
  static Future<void> exportToTxt(BuildContext context, Book book) async {
    try {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('姝ｅ湪鐢熸垚 TXT 鏂囦欢...'), duration: Duration(milliseconds: 1000)),
      );

      final isar = DatabaseService().isar;
      final chapters = await isar.chapters
          .filter()
          .bookIdEqualTo(book.id)
          .sortByOrderIndex()
          .findAll();

      if (chapters.isEmpty) {
        _showError(context, '杩欐湰涔﹁繕娌℃湁绔犺妭');
        return;
      }

      final StringBuffer buffer = StringBuffer();
      buffer.writeln("銆?{book.title}銆?);
      buffer.writeln("浣滆€咃細${book.authorName ?? '浣氬悕'}");
      buffer.writeln("\n" + ("=" * 20) + "\n");

      for (var chapter in chapters) {
        buffer.writeln(chapter.title);
        buffer.writeln("-" * 20);
        buffer.writeln(chapter.content);
        buffer.writeln("\n\n");
      }

      await _saveFileToDisk(context, book.title, 'txt', buffer.toString().codeUnits);

    } catch (e) {
      _showError(context, 'TXT 瀵煎嚭澶辫触: $e');
    }
  }

  // 2. 銆愭柊澧炪€慐PUB 瀵煎嚭閫昏緫
  static Future<void> exportToEpub(BuildContext context, Book book) async {
    try {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('姝ｅ湪鐢熸垚 EPUB 鐢靛瓙涔?..'), duration: Duration(seconds: 2)),
      );

      // 1. 鑾峰彇绔犺妭鏁版嵁
      final isar = DatabaseService().isar;
      final chapters = await isar.chapters
          .filter()
          .bookIdEqualTo(book.id)
          .sortByOrderIndex()
          .findAll();

      if (chapters.isEmpty) {
        _showError(context, '杩欐湰涔﹁繕娌℃湁绔犺妭');
        return;
      }

      // 2. 璋冪敤 EpubBuilder 鐢熸垚浜岃繘鍒舵暟鎹?
      final epubBytes = await EpubBuilder.build(book, chapters);

      // 3. 淇濆瓨鏂囦欢
      if (context.mounted) {
        await _saveFileToDisk(context, book.title, 'epub', epubBytes);
      }

    } catch (e) {
      _showError(context, 'EPUB 瀵煎嚭澶辫触: $e');
      debugPrint(e.toString());
    }
  }

  // --- 閫氱敤淇濆瓨閫昏緫 (澶嶇敤) ---
  static Future<void> _saveFileToDisk(BuildContext context, String title, String ext, List<int> bytes) async {
    // 1. 鍐欏叆涓存椂鏂囦欢
    final directory = await getTemporaryDirectory();
    final safeTitle = title.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[\\/:*?"<>|]'), '_');
    final fileName = "$safeTitle.$ext";
    final tempFile = File('${directory.path}/$fileName');

    await tempFile.writeAsBytes(bytes);

    if (!context.mounted) return;

    // 2. 璋冭捣绯荤粺淇濆瓨
    final params = SaveFileDialogParams(
      sourceFilePath: tempFile.path,
      fileName: fileName,
    );

    final filePath = await FlutterFileDialog.saveFile(params: params);

    if (context.mounted && filePath != null) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text('鉁?$ext 瀵煎嚭鎴愬姛锛?), behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating),
      );
    }
  }

  static void _showError(BuildContext context, String msg) {
    if (context.mounted) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text(msg), backgroundColor: Colors.red),
      );
    }
  }
}

import 'package:isar/isar.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/models/prompt_template.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/services/database_service.dart';

/// 鎻愮ず璇嶇鐞嗗櫒
/// 璐熻矗 Prompt 鐨勮幏鍙栥€佹嫾鎺ャ€侀噸缃拰鏇存柊
class PromptManager {

  static const String SCENE_CHAR_Extract = 'char_extract';
  static const String SCENE_CHAR_ANALYSIS = 'char_analysis';
  static const String SCENE_OUTLINE_NODE = 'outline_node'; // 銆愭柊澧炪€戠珷鑺傜粏绾茬敓鎴?
  static const String SCENE_WORLD_SETTING = 'world_setting'; // 銆愭柊澧炪€戜笘鐣岃/璁惧畾琛ュ叏

  /// 鑾峰彇鏋勫缓濂界殑 System Prompt (涓夋槑娌绘垚鍝?
  static Future<String> getSystemPrompt(String sceneCode) async {
    final isar = DatabaseService().isar;
    PromptTemplate? template = await isar.promptTemplates
        .filter()
        .sceneCodeEqualTo(sceneCode)
        .findFirst();

    if (template == null) {
      template = await _initDefaultTemplate(sceneCode);
    }

    if (template.isAdvancedMode && template.fullOverride != null) {
      return template.fullOverride!;
    }

    final buffer = StringBuffer();
    if (template.baseSystemPrompt != null) buffer.writeln(template.baseSystemPrompt!);
    if (template.userCustomPreference != null && template.userCustomPreference!.isNotEmpty) {
      buffer.writeln("\n銆愮敤鎴烽澶栬姹傘€慭n${template.userCustomPreference!}");
    }
    if (template.formatConstraint != null) buffer.writeln("\n${template.formatConstraint!}");

    return buffer.toString();
  }

  /// 鍒濆鍖栭粯璁ゆā鏉?
  static Future<PromptTemplate> _initDefaultTemplate(String sceneCode) async {

      template.formatConstraint = '''
杩斿洖 JSON 鏁扮粍锛屾牸寮忓涓嬶細

  {"name": "瑙掕壊鍚?, "description": "鏈珷琛ㄧ幇绠€浠?}

涓嶈杈撳嚭 markdown 浠ｇ爜鍧楋紝鐩存帴杩斿洖 JSON 瀛楃涓层€?'';
      template.userCustomPreference = "";
    }
    else if (sceneCode == SCENE_CHAR_ANALYSIS) {
      template.label = "瑙掕壊娣卞害鍒嗘瀽";
      template.baseSystemPrompt = "浣犳槸涓€涓弗璋ㄧ殑灏忚鍔╂墜銆傝闃呰鐢ㄦ埛鎻愪緵鐨勫皬璇存鏂囩墖娈碉紝鎬荤粨鎸囧畾瑙掕壊鐨勪俊鎭€?;
      template.formatConstraint = '''

    else if (sceneCode == SCENE_OUTLINE_NODE) {

      template.baseSystemPrompt = "浣犳槸涓€涓綉鏂囧ぇ绾茬瓥鍒掑笀銆傝闃呰褰撳墠绔犺妭姝ｆ枃锛屾彁鐐煎嚭鏍稿績鍓ф儏鑺傜偣銆?;

  "content": "鍓ф儏姊楁锛?00瀛椾互鍐咃紝姒傛嫭璧锋壙杞悎锛?
}
涓嶈杈撳嚭 markdown 浠ｇ爜鍧楋紝鐩存帴杩斿洖 JSON 瀛楃涓层€?'';
      template.userCustomPreference = "";
    }
    // 銆愭柊澧炪€戜笘鐣岃/璁惧畾琛ュ叏
    else if (sceneCode == SCENE_WORLD_SETTING) {
      template.label = "璁惧畾/涓栫晫瑙傝ˉ鍏?;
      template.baseSystemPrompt = "浣犳槸涓€涓笘鐣岃鏋舵瀯甯堛€傝闃呰灏忚姝ｆ枃锛屾彁鍙栨垨琛ュ厖鐩稿叧鐨勮瀹氫俊鎭€?;
      template.formatConstraint = "璇风洿鎺ヨ繑鍥炶ˉ鍏呭悗鐨勮瀹氭枃鏈紝涓嶈鍖呭惈 JSON 鏍煎紡锛屼篃涓嶈鍖呭惈鈥滃ソ鐨勨€濄€佲€滀互涓嬫槸璁惧畾鈥濈瓑搴熻瘽銆傜洿鎺ヨ緭鍑哄唴瀹广€?;
      template.userCustomPreference = "濡傛灉鏄鐜版湁璁惧畾鐨勮ˉ鍏咃紝璇蜂繚鎸侀鏍间竴鑷淬€?;
    }

    final isar = DatabaseService().isar;
    await isar.writeTxn(() async {
      await isar.promptTemplates.put(template);
    });

    return template;
  }

  static Future<void> updateUserPreference(String sceneCode, String preference) async {
    final isar = DatabaseService().isar;
    var template = await isar.promptTemplates.filter().sceneCodeEqualTo(sceneCode).findFirst();
    if (template != null) {
      await isar.writeTxn(() async {
        template.userCustomPreference = preference;
        template.updatedAt = DateTime.now();
        await isar.promptTemplates.put(template);
      });
    }
  }

  static Future<void> toggleAdvancedMode(String sceneCode, bool isAdvanced) async {
    final isar = DatabaseService().isar;
    var template = await isar.promptTemplates.filter().sceneCodeEqualTo(sceneCode).findFirst();
    if (template != null) {
      await isar.writeTxn(() async {
        template.isAdvancedMode = isAdvanced;
        if (isAdvanced && (template.fullOverride == null || template.fullOverride!.isEmpty)) {
          final currentSandwich = "${template.baseSystemPrompt}\n\n${template.userCustomPreference}\n\n${template.formatConstraint}";
          template.fullOverride = currentSandwich;
        }
        await isar.promptTemplates.put(template);
      });
    }
  }

  static Future<void> updateFullOverride(String sceneCode, String content) async {
    final isar = DatabaseService().isar;
    var template = await isar.promptTemplates.filter().sceneCodeEqualTo(sceneCode).findFirst();
    if (template != null) {
      await isar.writeTxn(() async {
        template.fullOverride = content;
        await isar.promptTemplates.put(template);
      });
    }
  }

  static Future<PromptTemplate> getTemplate(String sceneCode) async {
    final isar = DatabaseService().isar;
    var template = await isar.promptTemplates.filter().sceneCodeEqualTo(sceneCode).findFirst();
    return template ?? await _initDefaultTemplate(sceneCode);
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/utils/color_generator.dart ---
============================================================
import 'dart:math';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class ColorGenerator {
  /// 棰勮鐨勪竴缁勪綆楗卞拰搴︺€侀珮鏄庡害鐨勯┈鍗￠緳鑹茬郴锛岄€傚悎鍋氳儗鏅?
  static const List<Color> _bgColors = [
    Color(0xFFE8F5E9), // 娴呯豢
    Color(0xFFE3F2FD), // 娴呰摑
    Color(0xFFF3E5F5), // 娴呯传
    Color(0xFFFFF3E0), // 娴呮
    Color(0xFFFFEBEE), // 娴呯孩
    Color(0xFFE0F7FA), // 闈掕壊
    Color(0xFFFFF8E1), // 娴呴粍
    Color(0xFFF1F8E9), // 瀚╃豢
  ];

  /// 鏍规嵁瀛楃涓茬敓鎴愬浐瀹氱殑鑳屾櫙鑹?
  static Color generateBgColor(String input) {
    if (input.isEmpty) return _bgColors[0];
    final int hash = input.hashCode;
    final int index = hash.abs() % _bgColors.length;
    return _bgColors[index];
  }

  /// 鏍规嵁鑳屾櫙鑹茶绠楀悎閫傜殑鏂囧瓧棰滆壊 (閫氬父鏄繁鑹?
  static Color getTextColor(Color bgColor) {
    return HSLColor.fromColor(bgColor).withLightness(0.3).toColor();
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/utils/epub_builder.dart ---

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';
import 'package:archive/archive.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/models/book.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/models/chapter.dart';
import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;

class EpubBuilder {
  /// 鐢熸垚 EPUB 鏂囦欢浜岃繘鍒舵暟鎹?
  /// 銆愬叧閿慨澶嶃€戞柟娉曞悕鏀逛负 build锛岄€傞厤 ExportService 鐨勮皟鐢?
  static List<int> build(Book book, List<Chapter> chapters) {
    final archive = Archive();

    // 1. mimetype (蹇呴』鏄涓€涓枃浠?
    const mimetypeData = 'application/epub+zip';
    // archive 4.x 鍐欐硶锛氱洿鎺ユ坊鍔狅紝涓嶈缃?compress 灞炴€?
    archive.addFile(ArchiveFile('mimetype', mimetypeData.length, utf8.encode(mimetypeData)));

    // 2. META-INF/container.xml
    const containerXml = '''<?xml version="1.0"?>
<container version="1.0" xmlns="urn:oasis:names:tc:opendocument:xmlns:container">
  <rootfiles>
    <rootfile full-path="OEBPS/content.opf" media-type="application/oebps-package+xml"/>
  </rootfiles>
</container>''';
    archive.addFile(ArchiveFile('META-INF/container.xml', containerXml.length, utf8.encode(containerXml)));

    // 3. OEBPS 鐩綍鍐呭
    // 3.1 灏侀潰鍥剧墖 (濡傛灉鏈?
    String? coverFileName;
    if (book.coverPath != null) {
      final coverFile = File(book.coverPath!);
      if (coverFile.existsSync()) {
        final coverBytes = coverFile.readAsBytesSync();
        coverFileName = 'cover${p.extension(book.coverPath!)}';
        archive.addFile(ArchiveFile('OEBPS/images/$coverFileName', coverBytes.length, coverBytes));
      }
    }

    // 3.2 绔犺妭鍐呭 (XHTML)
    final manifestItems = <String>[];
    final spineItems = <String>[];

    // 娣诲姞灏侀潰椤?
    if (coverFileName != null) {
      final coverPage = '''<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml">
<head><title>Cover</title></head>
<body><div style="text-align: center; padding: 0pt; margin: 0pt;"><img src="images/$coverFileName" style="height: 100%;" /></div></body>
</html>''';
      archive.addFile(ArchiveFile('OEBPS/cover.xhtml', coverPage.length, utf8.encode(coverPage)));
      manifestItems.add('<item id="cover" href="cover.xhtml" media-type="application/xhtml+xml"/>');
      manifestItems.add('<item id="cover-image" href="images/$coverFileName" media-type="image/jpeg"/>');
      spineItems.add('<itemref idref="cover"/>');
    }

    // 娣诲姞绔犺妭
    for (int i = 0; i < chapters.length; i++) {
      final chapter = chapters[i];
      final chapterFileName = 'chapter_$i.xhtml';
      // 绠€鍗曠殑 HTML 澶勭悊锛屽鐞嗘崲琛?
      final contentHtml = chapter.content.split('\n').map((line) => line.trim().isNotEmpty ? '<p>$line</p>' : '').join('');

      final xhtml = '''<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml">
<head><title>${chapter.title}</title></head>
<body>
<h1>${chapter.title}</h1>
$contentHtml
</body>
</html>''';

      archive.addFile(ArchiveFile('OEBPS/$chapterFileName', xhtml.length, utf8.encode(xhtml)));
      manifestItems.add('<item id="chap$i" href="$chapterFileName" media-type="application/xhtml+xml"/>');
      spineItems.add('<itemref idref="chap$i"/>');
    }

    // 3.3 content.opf
    final opfContent = '''<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<package xmlns="http://www.idpf.org/2007/opf" unique-identifier="BookId" version="2.0">
  <metadata xmlns:dc="http://purl.org/dc/elements/1.1/" xmlns:opf="http://www.idpf.org/2007/opf">
    <dc:title>${book.title}</dc:title>
    <dc:language>zh-CN</dc:language>
    <meta name="cover" content="cover-image" />
  </metadata>
  <manifest>
    <item id="ncx" href="toc.ncx" media-type="application/x-dtbncx+xml"/>
    ${manifestItems.join('\n    ')}
  </manifest>
  <spine toc="ncx">
    ${spineItems.join('\n    ')}
  </spine>
</package>''';
    archive.addFile(ArchiveFile('OEBPS/content.opf', opfContent.length, utf8.encode(opfContent)));

    // 3.4 toc.ncx (鐩綍)
    final navPoints = List.generate(chapters.length, (i) {
      return '''<navPoint id="navPoint-${i + 1}" playOrder="${i + 1}">
      <navLabel><text>${chapters[i].title}</text></navLabel>
      <content src="chapter_$i.xhtml"/>
    </navPoint>''';
    }).join('\n    ');

    final ncxContent = '''<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<ncx xmlns="http://www.daisy.org/z3986/2005/ncx/" version="2005-1">
  <head><meta name="dtb:uid" content="urn:uuid:12345"/></head>
  <docTitle><text>${book.title}</text></docTitle>
  <navMap>
    $navPoints
  </navMap>
</ncx>''';
    archive.addFile(ArchiveFile('OEBPS/toc.ncx', ncxContent.length, utf8.encode(ncxContent)));

    // 4. 鎵撳寘
    final encoder = ZipEncoder();
    return encoder.encode(archive)!;
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/utils/highlight_text_editing_controller.dart ---

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class HighlightTextEditingController extends TextEditingController {
  // --- 涓存椂楂樹寒鐩稿叧锛堢敤浜?AI 鐢熸垚鍙嶉锛?---
  TextRange? _tempHighlightRange;
  Color? _tempHighlightColor;

  // --- 鍏抽敭璇嶉珮浜浉鍏筹紙鍘熸湁閫昏緫锛?---
  Set<String> _keywords = {};

  // 璁剧疆涓存椂楂樹寒锛堜緥濡傦細绱壊閿佸畾鍖?鎴?缁胯壊缁撴灉鍖猴級
  void setTemporaryHighlight(int start, int end, Color color) {
    if (start >= end) return;
    _tempHighlightRange = TextRange(start: start, end: end);
    _tempHighlightColor = color;
    notifyListeners(); // 瑙﹀彂閲嶇粯
  }

  // 娓呴櫎涓存椂楂樹寒
  void clearTemporaryHighlight() {
    if (_tempHighlightRange != null) {
      _tempHighlightRange = null;
      _tempHighlightColor = null;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  // 鏇存柊鍏抽敭璇嶏紙鍘熸湁鍔熻兘淇濇寔涓嶅彉锛?
  void updateKeywords(Set<String> newKeywords) {
    _keywords = newKeywords;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  @override
  TextSpan buildTextSpan({
    required BuildContext context,
    TextStyle? style,
    required bool withComposing,
  }) {
    // 1. 濡傛灉娌℃湁浠讳綍楂樹寒闇€姹傦紝鐩存帴杩斿洖鏅€氭枃鏈紝淇濇寔楂樻€ц兘
    if ((_tempHighlightRange == null) && _keywords.isEmpty) {
      return super.buildTextSpan(context: context, style: style, withComposing: withComposing);
    }

    final List<TextSpan> children = [];
    final String text = this.text;

    // 绠€鍗曡捣瑙侊紝鎴戜滑浼樺厛澶勭悊銆愪复鏃堕珮浜€戯紝瀹冪殑灞傜骇楂樹簬鍏抽敭璇?
    // 濡傛灉瀛樺湪涓存椂楂樹寒锛屾垜浠妸鏂囨湰鍒囨垚涓夋锛歔鏅€歖 [楂樹寒] [鏅€歖
    // (鍏抽敭璇嶉珮浜粎鍦ㄦ櫘閫氭钀戒腑鐢熸晥锛屾殏涓嶈€冭檻鍙犲姞澶嶆潅鎯呭喌)

    int currentIndex = 0;

    if (_tempHighlightRange != null) {
      final int start = _tempHighlightRange!.start.clamp(0, text.length);
      final int end = _tempHighlightRange!.end.clamp(0, text.length);

      // 绗竴娈碉細楂樹寒鍓嶇殑鏂囨湰
      if (start > currentIndex) {
        children.add(_buildKeywordSpan(
          text.substring(currentIndex, start),
          style,
        ));
      }

      // 绗簩娈碉細楂樹寒鏂囨湰 (搴旂敤鑳屾櫙鑹?
      if (end > start) {
        children.add(TextSpan(
          text: text.substring(start, end),
          style: style?.copyWith(
            backgroundColor: _tempHighlightColor,
          ),
        ));
      }

      // 鏇存柊绱㈠紩
      currentIndex = end;
    }

    // 绗笁娈?鍓╀綑鏂囨湰
    if (currentIndex < text.length) {
      children.add(_buildKeywordSpan(
        text.substring(currentIndex),
        style,
      ));
    }

    return TextSpan(style: style, children: children);
  }

  // 杈呭姪鏂规硶锛氬鐞嗗叧閿瘝楂樹寒锛堝鐢ㄦ偍鍘熸湁鐨勯€昏緫鎬濊矾锛?
  TextSpan _buildKeywordSpan(String textSegment, TextStyle? style) {
    if (_keywords.isEmpty) return TextSpan(text: textSegment, style: style);

    final List<TextSpan> spans = [];
    final pattern = RegExp(_keywords.map(RegExp.escape).join('|'));

    textSegment.splitMapJoin(
      pattern,
      onMatch: (m) {
        spans.add(TextSpan(
          text: m[0],
          style: style?.copyWith(
            color: Colors.blueAccent, // 鍏抽敭璇嶉鑹蹭繚鎸佸師鏈夎瀹?
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
          ),
        ));
        return '';
      },
      onNonMatch: (n) {
        spans.add(TextSpan(text: n, style: style));
        return '';
      },
    );

    return TextSpan(children: spans);
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/widgets/monet_avatar.dart ---

import 'dart:io';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class MonetAvatar extends StatelessWidget {
  final String? avatarPath;
  final String? name; // 鐢ㄤ簬鏃犲浘鏃舵樉绀洪瀛?(鍙€?
  final double size; // 鐩村緞
  final String? heroTag;
  final VoidCallback? onTap;
  final bool showBorder;

  const MonetAvatar({
    super.key,
    this.avatarPath,
    this.name,
    this.size = 40,
    this.heroTag,
    this.onTap,
    this.showBorder = false,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final hasImage = avatarPath != null && File(avatarPath!).existsSync();

    Widget avatar = Container(
      width: size,
      height: size,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        shape: BoxShape.circle,
        color: hasImage ? Colors.transparent : theme.colorScheme.surfaceContainerHighest,
        border: showBorder ? Border.all(color: Colors.white, width: 2) : null,
        image: hasImage
            ? DecorationImage(
          image: FileImage(File(avatarPath!)),
          fit: BoxFit.cover,
        )
            : null,
      ),
      alignment: Alignment.center,
      child: !hasImage
          ? (name != null && name!.isNotEmpty
          ? Text(name![0], style: TextStyle(fontSize: size * 0.4, color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant))
          : Icon(Icons.person, size: size * 0.6, color: Colors.grey))
          : null,
    );

    if (heroTag != null) {
      avatar = Hero(tag: heroTag!, child: avatar);
    }

    if (onTap != null) {
      return GestureDetector(onTap: onTap, child: avatar);
    }

    return avatar;
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/widgets/monet_book_cover.dart ---
============================================================
import 'dart:io';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:monet_writer/models/book.dart'; // 銆愭柊澧炪€戝紩鍏?Book 妯″瀷
import 'package:monet_writer/utils/color_generator.dart';

class MonetBookCover extends StatelessWidget {
  // 銆愭柊澧炪€戞帴鏀?Book 瀵硅薄
  final Book? book;

  // 鍘熸湁鍙傛暟鏀逛负鍙€夛紝淇濇寔鍚戝悗鍏煎
  final String? coverPath;
  final String? title;

  final double width;
  final double height;
  final String? heroTag;
  final double radius;
  final BoxShadow? shadow;

  const MonetBookCover({
    super.key,
    this.book,      // 銆愭柊澧炪€?
    this.coverPath, // 鏀逛负鍙€?
    this.title,     // 鏀逛负鍙€?
    this.width = 60,
    this.height = 90,
    this.heroTag,
    this.radius = 6,
    this.shadow,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // 銆愭牳蹇冮€昏緫銆戜紭鍏堜娇鐢?book 瀵硅薄鐨勬暟鎹紝濡傛灉娌℃湁鍒欎娇鐢ㄥ崟鐙紶鍏ョ殑鍙傛暟
    final String effectiveTitle = book?.title ?? title ?? '鏃犳爣棰?;
    final String? effectiveCoverPath = book?.coverPath ?? coverPath;

    // 1. 鍩虹瀹瑰櫒
    Widget coverContent = Container(
      width: width,
      height: height,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: ColorGenerator.generateBgColor(effectiveTitle), // 鍔ㄦ€佽儗鏅?
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(radius),
        boxShadow: [
          shadow ?? BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.05),
            blurRadius: 4,
            offset: const Offset(0, 2),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,
      child: _buildImageOrPlaceholder(effectiveCoverPath, effectiveTitle),
    );

    // 2. 濡傛灉閰嶇疆浜?Hero 鍔ㄧ敾
    if (heroTag != null) {
      return Hero(tag: heroTag!, child: coverContent);
    }

    return coverContent;
  }

  Widget _buildImageOrPlaceholder(String? path, String displayTitle) {
    final hasImage = path != null && File(path).existsSync();

    if (hasImage) {
      return Image.file(
        File(path!),
        fit: BoxFit.cover,
        errorBuilder: (_, __, ___) => _buildPlaceholder(displayTitle),
      );
    }
    return _buildPlaceholder(displayTitle);
  }

  Widget _buildPlaceholder(String displayTitle) {
    final bgColor = ColorGenerator.generateBgColor(displayTitle);
    final textColor = ColorGenerator.getTextColor(bgColor);

    // 瀛椾綋澶у皬鏍规嵁瀹藉害鑷€傚簲
    final fontSize = width * 0.4;

    return Center(
      child: Text(
        displayTitle.isNotEmpty ? displayTitle[0] : '鏃?,
        style: TextStyle(
          color: textColor,
          fontSize: fontSize,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/widgets/monet_text_field.dart ---

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class MonetTextField extends StatelessWidget {
  final TextEditingController controller;
  final String label;
  final String? hint;
  final IconData? icon;

  // 銆愪慨澶嶃€戞敼涓哄彲绌?int锛屽厑璁镐紶 null 瀹炵幇鑷€傚簲楂樺害
  final int? maxLines;
  final int minLines;

  final bool autoFocus;
  final TextInputType? keyboardType;
  final Function(String)? onChanged;

  const MonetTextField({
    super.key,
    required this.controller,
    required this.label,
    this.hint,
    this.icon,
    this.maxLines = 1, // 榛樿涓?1
    this.minLines = 1,
    this.autoFocus = false,
    this.keyboardType,
    this.onChanged,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    return TextField(
      controller: controller,
      maxLines: maxLines,
      minLines: minLines,
      autofocus: autoFocus,
      keyboardType: keyboardType,
      style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16),
      onChanged: onChanged,
      decoration: InputDecoration(
        labelText: label,
        hintText: hint,
        prefixIcon: icon != null ? Icon(icon, size: 20) : null,
        filled: true,
        fillColor: theme.colorScheme.surfaceContainerHighest.withOpacity(0.3),
        border: _buildBorder(),
        enabledBorder: _buildBorder(color: Colors.transparent),
        focusedBorder: _buildBorder(color: theme.colorScheme.primary, width: 2),
        contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 16),
        isDense: true,
      ),
    );
  }

  OutlineInputBorder _buildBorder({Color color = Colors.transparent, double width = 0.0}) {
    return OutlineInputBorder(
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
      borderSide: BorderSide(color: color, width: width),
    );
  }
}
